# PRD: Chats (WhatsApp via Haberchat)

**Status:** Live
**Last updated:** 2026-10-06 (**A new number that shows interest becomes a client and gets the agent.** A WhatsApp message that is not from an ad, from a number that is not a client and not a saved contact / project officer / advertiser / real-estate office (`wa_phone_known_party`, migration `2026-10-06_01`), and that names one of our projects, looks for a property, or sends a website unit code → the client is created from the chat (name = WhatsApp name, source واتساب) and the sales agent answers (a unit code still gets its PDF from the bot this time; the agent takes the next message). Not when a rep is talking in the chat, the assistant is paused, or the AI is off. A notice lands in the AI notifications. Before this such numbers only ever got the basic bot. Code: `api/whatsapp/basic-reply.ts` (`onboardInterestedNewNumber`, `showsInterest`), `escalation.ts` (`ensureClientForChat`).) | 2026-10-05 (**Visit-detail questions go to the project officer.** A customer's question about the DETAILS of visiting a project — is the guard / sales office there, can someone open it and show them around, working hours, coming without an appointment, who receives them, parking/access — no longer becomes a «سؤال من المساعد الآلي» card for the rep: the agent's new `ask_project_officer` tool drafts a message to the project's officer (AI tab, waits for approval like every officer message; the officer contacts the customer). WHEN-can-I-visit questions stay with the agent (offer a time, book_visit). No officer on record → it asks the rep as before. Code: `brain.ts` (rule 7b, tool), `turn.ts` (`askOfficer` → `draftOfficerQuestion`, trigger `visit_question`).) | 2026-10-05 (**The sales agent works each preference profile separately.** A client with several profiles (one per property — e.g. a villa to live in and an apartment for their son) is shown to the agent as a list with ids; every search_projects names the one it is for (`profile_id`) and uses that profile's values and places (`saved_area`), never mixing two. With several profiles the whole-chat preference reading is not applied to searches (it mixes the wishes). The chat's «لقيت» card shows the newest result for each profile under its name, and each search carries «لـ «اسم التفضيل»». Key files: `api/_lib/salesAgent/savedProfile.ts` (`profilesLine`), `brain.ts` (rule 6h, `profile_id`), `turn.ts`, `src/pages/Chats/components/AiActivityPanel.tsx`.) | 2026-10-05 (**AI cards fold like the top section.** The «ما فعله الذكاء الاصطناعي» panel gained the same chevron fold button as the chat's top section; before, only its title folded it, which was easy to miss.) | 2026-10-05 (**Auto-save is profile-aware.** A chat read routes what it heard before saving: same wish → merge; change of mind → replace the AI's old values for the changed fields; separate second wish → a new preference profile (not made active). Places wanted and excluded at once can no longer happen (newer wins). See clients.md for the details.) | 2026-10-05 (**The chat AI reads WhatsApp tasks only.** Its outcome readings attach to the client's open WhatsApp task, never a call task — call results are set by a person) | 2026-10-05 (**The chats on a phone, checked button by button.** A new crawler (`npm run phone-check:chats`) opens the list and a conversation at iPhone and Android size and taps every button and every button inside what it opens, with every write held back. Fixes from it: an open conversation is full-screen on a phone (the app bar is hidden; the chat's own back arrow returns to the list); the chat page's rows load first when the app opens on it (~1 s instead of waiting ~5 s for every model); WhatsApp media that is gone for good (404/410) is shown as unavailable once instead of being re-downloaded on every visit; a failed status change / archive no longer throws an unhandled error and its database write is reverted along with the screen; the top bar says «المحادثات» instead of «تعديل السجل…»; the small controls (list header icons, number and kind filters, status pill, AI card tabs and collapse, «اكتب رداً», refresh, undo, project-message actions) are finger-sized on phones, unchanged on desktop.)
**Last updated:** 2026-10-05 (**Specialized search when nothing fits.** The sales agent now sees a checklist of what a specialized search (an unanswered request) still needs — unit type, a district, one of budget / bedrooms / size — and, when nothing we have fits, offers the customer a «بحث خاص» and asks only for the missing pieces; see unanswered-requests.md.) | 2026-10-05 (**One number, one card.** Settings → WhatsApp → Numbers drew every live number twice — a connection card at the top and a settings row below — plus the main line's retired `wassel_main` session as a fourth «منقطع» number: 7 cards for 3 lines. Each active number on a WAHA session is now ONE card: live status, restart / re-pair, and its own controls (default / operations badges, rename, set default, operations, hide) inside it. Hidden numbers fold under «أرقام مخفية (n)»; `wassel_main` is kept, hidden, so the old chats that point at it keep a name.)
**Last updated:** 2026-10-05 (**A change of mind wins over older messages.** The shared preference reader is authoritative for the search, and it read the whole chat — so «خلاص غيرت رأيي، ابي دور في ظهرة لبن» was searched as شقة from an earlier «ابي شقة…». Now a restart («انسى اللي قبل», «غيرت رأيي») limits the reader to the messages from there on, and a value whose quote is only in older messages (not in the customer's messages since our last reply) no longer replaces what the agent read now; it still fills a field the agent left empty, and the agent's state line marks it as from earlier messages.)
**Last updated:** 2026-10-05 (**No match → the closest real options, labelled.** With only our 96 projects, a strict ask («شقة جاهزة 3 غرف شمال الرياض قريبة من مترو بمليون و200») found nothing at every step, because the search never widens the place condition past twice the distance and never drops ready / off-plan — and the agent answered «ما عندنا» while مينا 51 (ready, 2.5 km from a station) and مكانة (0.5 km, off-plan) were there. When nothing fits, `searchProjects` now returns `alternatives`: the same search inside the same area once without the place (nearest first, real distances) and once without ready / off-plan; when neither finds anything and an area was asked, once outside the area (an area with none of our projects — «دور في ظهرة لبن» — got nothing while 3-room أدوار were elsewhere in Riyadh). The agent offers the best one saying exactly what differs, and the chat's «لقيت» card lists them under «لا شيء بكل الشروط — الأقرب بدون …». Each project's fitting units also carry the cheapest price per room count (`fit.from_by_bedrooms`) and the agent must quote the price of the room count it names — the first live run said «تبدأ من مليون و40 (غرفتين و3 غرف)» while 3-room units started at 1,348,000.)
**Last updated:** 2026-10-05 (**The agent's area search looks at OUR projects only.** `sales_agent_geo_match` matched the customer's area against every geolocated record — 1,028 project points AND 295,630 archived market-listing points — and «غرب الرياض بس، مو الشرق» hit the 20 s timeout, so the agent fell back to «كم غرفة تبي؟» after 134 s. It now uses `wassell_geo_match_projects` (project points only, same rules; migration `2026-10-05_03`): the same area takes 1.4 s. Later the same day, operator: the area search looks ONLY at our projects (the 96 `our_projects` records, through each one's master `all_projects` point) — `wassell_geo_match_our_projects`, migration `2026-10-05_04`; «غرب الرياض، مو الشرق» now takes 0.1 s and returns فلل رِفان, the one project of ours in the west.)
**Last updated:** 2026-10-05 (**Live agent test fixes.** (1) «فيلا في النرجس… قريبة من محطة مترو» lost the metro condition — reading the area from the chat erased `near`; it is now kept as an extra condition on top of the area, and the prompt says so. (2) The reply guard refuses a distance / nearness claim about a place («قريبة من محطة مترو، أقل من كيلو») when no search this turn measured distances — the agent had told the customer «أقل من كيلو» while the nearest station was 5.8–8.1 km away. (3) Floor-plan images are sent with the type their bytes say (a PNG stored as image/jpeg was refused and 6 plans went unchecked). (4) Chips: «حي» is no longer doubled («حي حي النرجس») or put in front of a zone; a saved zone is labelled with the reader's Arabic token («شمال الرياض»), not the customer's own wording (an English «north Riyadh» had become the label of 36 districts).) | 2026-10-05 (**An ad lead's question goes to the agent, not the fixed hand-off line.** For an ad click whose opener is a real question («فين موقعكم بأي حي», «وين الموقع بالضبط»), the basic bot's Kimi pre-check used to answer «أبشر، بيتواصل معك زميلي…» and stop — the agent never saw it (3 real buyers in 7 days). The classifier now has a separate `not_customer` answer (vendor pitch, job seeker, wrong number, spam), which keeps the line + alert; any other customer question from an ad lead starts the sales agent with the ad's project, which answers it and hands to a rep itself when it must.) | 2026-10-05 (**AI activity cards replace the reply box (operator, 2026-10-05).** In a client chat the AI is answering, the space under the thread now shows what the AI did instead of the composer: summary (AI / customer / rep message counts, runs, projects sent, visits booked), what it understood (the shared reader's reading), its searches (criteria, how many it found, whether it widened), what it found (top projects of the latest search, «أُرسل» on the ones sent), what it did (projects / units sent, visits booked, questions to a rep, handoffs, portal registrations, officer notices, follow-up drafts), what it changed on the client (the AI-changes list with Undo) and the run log (each turn: customer text, reply, checker rejections, tool trace, seconds). «اكتب رداً» opens the composer for that chat; «عرض بطاقات الذكاء الاصطناعي» goes back. Chats where a rep stopped the AI, groups, the operations line and non-client chats keep the composer. Every agent turn is now recorded in `wa_agent_runs` (was a console line only) — so the run cards are empty for turns before 2026-10-05.) | 2026-10-05 (**The agent reads the customer's wants with the SAME extractor as the profile (operator, 2026-10-05).** Each WhatsApp agent turn runs the shared preference extractor (`api/_lib/prefExtract.ts`, via `api/_lib/salesAgent/prefReading.ts`) over the current conversation, with the same customer-quote guard as the profile save. Its unit type, budget, bedrooms and size are AUTHORITATIVE in every `search_projects` (`applyCustomerReading` — the agent's own value is replaced and the change shows in the trace as «reader →»); fields it didn't read keep the agent's value (e.g. from the saved profile). Shown to the agent as a state line. Read-only (the background reader still saves the profile). Adds ~2 s (measured 1.8–2.6 s); capped at 9 s — a slow or failed reading never blocks the reply, the agent then uses its own reading and the failure is logged. Dry runs: «شقة ٣ غرف … ٩٠٠ ألف» → apartment / 3+ / ≤900k applied; villa→«دور بحدود مليون ونص» → دور ≤1.5M (latest wish); a townhouse the rep offered but the customer didn't take was ignored.) | 2026-10-04 (**The client profile fills itself in; results apply themselves (operator, 2026-10-04).** After a chat or call reading, the AI saves what it found onto the client with no rep tick (`api/_lib/clientPrefs/autoSave.ts`, switch `ai_automation_settings.auto_save_profile`): preferences only when the quote is in the customer's own messages; sets (unit type, purpose, amenities) are added to, never removed; a range (budget, area, bedrooms) is written when empty or when the AI wrote the current value — a value a rep typed is kept and the heard value is shown as «سمع … — أبقى قيمتك»; calls stay fill-empty-only; places are saved only when resolved, savable and not doubted by the checker (doubted ones stay for the rep and are listed). Everything is logged in `client_ai_changes` and shown on the chat card as «حفظه الذكاء الاصطناعي في ملف العميل» with Undo per value (`/api/client-ai-changes`; refuses when a rep changed the value since). Follow-up results are recorded by the AI too (`api/_lib/outcomeAutoApply.ts`, step 3b of the 5-minute cron): confidence ≥ 80, chat quiet 15 min, no newer customer message, follow-up still open, and the popup's own completion validation passes — otherwise the reading stays for a person; readings for follow-ups already closed are retired. A positive result sets the main project only when the client has none and the customer's own words back it (an ad's opener button doesn't count; the outcome reader now ignores ad buttons too). A failed result reading is retried twice (2 min apart) instead of being final. **The WhatsApp agent uses the saved profile and the one geography engine (2026-10-04).** Each turn starts with the client's SAVED profile (`api/_lib/salesAgent/savedProfile.ts`): unit type, budget, bedrooms, size, purpose, places — the agent searches with it from the first message and never re-asks what it answers; `saved_area=true` searches the client's saved places through `sales_agent_geo_match`. A district or region the customer names now goes through the geography engine (`area_from_chat`) instead of the agent typing district names itself; a named place for «قريب من» goes through the engine's resolver (`resolveNamedPlace` in places.ts) instead of a fuzzy top-result search — a chain mall or a university with two campuses is asked about («أي فرع؟») instead of guessed. The engine's element search now folds ة/ه, ى/ي, أ/ا («غرناطة مول» finds «غرناطه مول»).) | 2026-10-04 (**A place said as a station resolves to the station.** When the customer says «محطة» / «مترو» / "station" with a venue — in the span («محطة مترو مركز الملك عبدالله المالي», «مترو العزيزية») or right before it in their words (the extractor often keeps only «مستشفى الإيمان» from «محطة مستشفى الإيمان») — anchorPrep sets `station` and the resolver looks the name up also without the station words and as «محطة X», and a metro station of that exact name wins over the same-named hospital / district / business area. The stripped name never picks a non-station. Without a station word nothing changes. Applies to the places card and the sales agent alike (the card still asks for a distance; the agent assumes 1 km). Live check: KAFD station, Al Iman Hospital station, Ad Douh, Al Aziziah → each the station; «قريبة من مستشفى الإيمان» unchanged. «محطة مترو العليا» still asks — there is no Olaya station on the map.) | 2026-10-04 (**«Near» with no distance — the agent assumes it.** When the WhatsApp sales agent reads an area from the chat (`area_from_chat`) and the customer names a place with no distance («قريبة من الرياض بارك»), it now uses 3 km for a landmark and 1 km for a metro station (operator decision) — the same numbers its near tool already used — and says the distance back. A stated distance always wins. Only the agent's reading does this (`default_near_radius_m`, set in geoGate.ts); the reps' places card still asks, so a guessed distance is never saved onto a client. Live check: «قريبة من الرياض بارك» → 3 km; «… بحدود ٥ كيلو» → 5 km; «قريبة من محطة الدوح» → 1 km.) | 2026-10-04 (**Card quality fixes (audit 2026-10-04).** (1) The ad's prefilled opener buttons — «مهتم بمشروع يمام 17» and «مهتم بمشاريع سكنية اخرى في شمال الرياض» (north / east / centre) — are no longer read as the customer's words: `gatherChatConversation` drops them (`isAdOpenerTemplate`, keywordGate.ts), so neither the places reader nor the preferences reader sees them, and the keyword gate treats the plural button as noise too. The region on that button is the AD's targeting; it had put "north Riyadh" on places cards and guessed unit type / purpose from the project name. The sales agent still reads it (isOtherProjectsAsk). Open suggestions minted from it were cleaned once: 17 places cards pruned (5 left empty → superseded), 13 preference cards (8 left empty → superseded). (2) A place the customer named twice is ONE row on the places card (`buildGeoRows` merges same place / side / rule and keeps every mention id, so un-ticking drops both) — 25 of 82 open cards repeated a line; `summarizeGeometry` dedupes the same way. (3) A places proposal with no place in it no longer appears in the review queue (`/api/geo-preference/proposals`); the chat card already hid it (18 of 82).) | 2026-10-04 (**WhatsApp sales agent fixes from three live chats.** (1) A turn now reads the agent's own replies still in scheduled_whatsapp_jobs (ai% references, last 15 min) as part of the conversation — a reply waits 10–15 s behind project media, and the next turn used to answer the same thing again in other words (two «أكنان 25…» lines 2 s apart). (2) «<no_reply>» anywhere in the model's answer means send nothing; a note after it failed the guard twice and fell back to the «بيتواصل معك زميلي» holding line for a customer who only said «تمام مشكور». (3) The guard checks spoken amounts whole («مليون و830 ألف» = 1,830,000 must match a price in the facts; a spoken amount in the customer's words is grounded too) — digit by digit only «830» was seen, so مليون-for-مليونين passed. (4) area_from_chat reads the CURRENT conversation's last 8 turns, not the whole chat (which had piled every place ever named into ~16,000 records). (5) A negotiation hand-off (discount, last price for cash, payment terms) also drafts an officer notice with the customer's exact question — or adds it to the notice already waiting for that client × officer (draftOfficerQuestion, approval-only).) | 2026-10-04 (**One chat per number, with a number switcher at the top of Chats.** «كل الأرقام» / All numbers, then one chip per active number — Sales «المبيعات» (the default), Operations «العمليات», and any other number by its display name, e.g. «طلبات العملاء» / Client requests for the office-outreach line. A contact who talked to two of our numbers is TWO chats: each with its own row, preview, unread count and thread, and replies leave from that chat's number. The contact is still one conversation record; the per-number state lives in `data.lines` + `data.line_meta`, kept by the webhook and back-filled from `chat_messages`.) | (**The WhatsApp sales agent reads described areas through the geography agent.** search_projects gains area_from_chat: when the customer describes an area in words — a side of a road («غرب طريق الملك فهد»), a district on one side of a road («النرجس شمال طريق الملك سلمان»), a distance they gave from a road or place, several areas at once — the agent sets it and api/_lib/salesAgent/geoGate.ts runs the SAME geography pipeline as the reps' places card over the whole chat (extract → resolve → compile → location_items, read-only: nothing saved, no client geometry touched), then sales_agent_geo_match (migration 2026-10-01_sales_agent_geo_match.sql, throwaway key) returns the projects inside. The catalog search keeps only those (relaxing to outside the area only when nothing inside fits, said as relaxed "area"); the tool result carries area_understood so the reply says back what was understood. An area that cannot be placed returns area_status "not_understood" and the agent asks one question instead of guessing. Plain district names and plain regions still use districts / zone; landmarks without a distance still use near. Tested before shipping in dry-run (real model + map, nothing sent): west of King Fahd Road → 11 projects in the band; Narjis north of King Salman → 8 (all Narjis); within 2 km of King Fahd Road → 10, each measured 0.01–1.95 km from the road; "north of King Fahd Road" (a north–south road) → asks. «النرجس أو شمال طريق الملك سلمان» first came out as Narjis-north-of-the-road only: geoGate did not pass the chat turns to the pipeline (ctx.conversation, as the places card does), so the «او» before the road side was invisible and the side was distributed onto Narjis. Fixed the same day, together with «او اي مكان شمال …» (filler words between «او» and the alternative); all three phrasings now give Narjis (14 projects, both sides of the road) OR the band north of King Salman Road (16 in total), while «النرجس شمال طريق الملك سلمان» still clips to the 8 north of the road.) | 2026-10-04 (**Interest: our projects only; a "no" keeps the mark.** (Migration 2026-10-04_05.) Appointments and visits add to the interest score only for OUR projects (a record in the our_projects model — 96 today; the project_type tag misses 21 of them). A customer saying a project does not suit them no longer zeroes the score: the links / appointment / visit points stay (they may come back to it), the message adds nothing, and the option's status becomes not interested; the detector does not raise a NEW high-interest event (portal registration) for a project whose latest message is a "no". **Appointments now pick from Our Projects** (migration 2026-10-04_06): appointments.project_id looks up our_projects, like visits.project_id (display = the project name through the mirror). The appointment form's project picker stores the Our Projects entry; the WhatsApp agent's book_visit saves the Our Projects entry of the project the customer agreed on (no refusals — a project that is not ours is saved without a project and the rep is told, as record_visit already did); the 13 existing appointments were converted, the old master id kept on each under project_id_all_projects (4 whose project is not ours now have no project); readers that wanted the master project (follow-up workspace context, follow-up writer, options card, interest view) map through the entry's `project`. The card's score chip no longer turns red on a "no" — the status badge carries it.) | 2026-10-04 (**The places card asks instead of guessing** when the customer's words do not pin a place down: a side that does not run along its road («شرق طريق الدمام», «شمال طريق الملك فهد»), a direction beside a road or a region, a distance the customer never said (or a travel time), a venue name that fits two places, «الرياض» that is really «الرياض بارك», or a city named elsewhere in the chat. Such a line shows as the usual "needs confirming" tile and is never saved. See geo-preference-ability.md.) | 2026-10-04 (**Messages count toward interest.** The outcome agent's project reactions gain a third kind, "asked" (real questions about THIS project — price, units, plan, location, delivery — no yes/no yet), quote-verified like the others. The worker writes every reaction to client_project_message_signals via client_project_message_signal (asked / wants / rejected; "asked" never downgrades "wants", "wants"/"rejected" replace — the customer can change their mind) as well as to the option (an "asked" project is just presented). ONE interest score per client + project (v_client_project_interest, 0–100, migration 2026-10-04_04): the link score + an appointment booked (+40) OR a visit done (+50, the larger, not both) + what the customer's own messages say (the outcome agent's reading: asked real questions +15, said it suits them +25; said it does not suit them → no message points, the rest of the score is KEPT and the option becomes not_interested). Weights are data (ai_automation_settings.interest_weights). The 5-minute high-interest detector (client_interest_detect_links, same name) now reads this score, so a booked appointment alone is high interest and registers the client in the developer's portal before the visit; a pair never gets a second interest event. The follow-up writer sees each option's combined score. The WhatsApp agent already books appointments (book_visit → appointment, status scheduled) and records visits the customer mentions (record_visit → visit; only counted when the project is one of our_projects).) | 2026-10-04 (**AI-sent projects land in the client's options; the AI keeps their status.** A project the AI sends on WhatsApp is added to the client's options (`added_from='ai'`, status presented) by the `tg_chat_message_projects_ai_option` trigger on chat_message_projects → `client_option_mark` (migration 2026-10-04_02_ai_client_options.sql; 144 past sends backfilled). The outcome agent also returns `project_reactions` — a project the customer clearly wanted or turned down, with the customer's own quote — and the worker applies each via `client_option_mark` (interested / not_interested) only when that quote is really in the customer's messages. It never overrides eliminated / reserved / closed, never downgrades interested, and a not-interested project loses its main flag. The follow-up writer now also reads the last three calls in full (diarized العميل/المندوب turns, else the summary), visits and appointments, the client's saved preferences, every option with its status and the AI's latest reading — and never brings up a project marked not interested.) | 2026-10-04 (**Outcome agent picks the MAIN project; follow-ups drafted by AI.** worker/src/runChatOutcomeJob.ts now gets the client's project options plus the projects sent to the chat and, on interested / appointment_booked / request_offer, returns the one the customer is most interested in (main_project, only from that list). chat_outcome_suggestion_ready stores suggested_main_project_id/_name and records HIGH INTEREST (client_project_interest) — which registers the client in the project's portal and drafts an officer notice. max_tokens 2000 → 8000 (14 of 44 readings had come back empty), and an empty reply cut off by the limit now fails with that reason. Due WhatsApp follow-ups get a message WRITTEN by the AI (api/_lib/salesAgent/followupDraft.ts: whole thread with real speaker labels, last calls, past outcomes, projects sent + link interest, the rep-voice rules, the numbers guard) held for approval in the Work Queue AI tab. chatIngest: an echo with no send_source is classed as the assistant's message when an ai:/ai-project: job for that chat is running or finished in the last minute (it used to read as a rep message and cancel call tasks).) | 2026-10-01 (**Sales agent: places, unit features and floor plans.** (1) **Places** — search_projects takes `near` (a named road / mall / university / hospital / landmark, or a kind of place such as any metro station, each with a max distance); `sales_agent_find_places` resolves the name in `geo_elements` (folds «أبو/أبي», prefers the kind the customer named), `sales_agent_project_distances` measures km from each project's own coordinates with PostGIS, results come nearest first with `distances_km`, and a place we can't find is reported back (`unresolved_places`) instead of the agent guessing districts. (2) **Features** — search_projects / search_units take `features` in the customer's words, mapped to the 29 stored `unit_components` (maid / driver room, roof, elevator, private entrance, fitted kitchen, concealed AC…; `features.ts`); a unit counts only when its recorded components include ALL of them. search_units also takes `floor_min` / `floor_max` / `exclude_floors` («above floor 15», «not the ground floor»). (3) **Floor plans** — a feature the data does not record (open kitchen, study room) comes back as `unknown_features`; the new `check_unit_plans` tool reads the distinct plan images of the matching units with Claude vision (at most 8 new plans per call, most-shared first), caches each answer in `unit_plan_checks`, and returns the unit ids whose plan shows it (sendable) plus no / unclear / unchecked / no-plan counts; anything unproven goes to ask_rep. Metered as `salesAgent/planCheck`. Migration: `2026-10-01_sales_agent_places_and_plan_checks.sql`.) | 2026-10-01 (**Sales agent finds the right homes, measured.** A retrieval-accuracy test (`scripts/agent-accuracy/` — 24 scenarios whose correct answer is computed straight from the live inventory: project hunts by district/type/bedrooms/budget/ready/size, unit hunts inside a named project by floor/cheapest/budget/size, multi-turn narrowing, two «nothing fits» traps, one English customer) went from 5/24 to 24/24 after four changes: (1) the project search now checks real AVAILABLE units — a project passes only when ONE unit meets every criterion together, not when its summary has 3-bedroom units and separately has units under the budget — and every result carries `fit` (count, price and size range of the units that match), which the agent quotes instead of the project's overall starting price (it used to say «from 899 ألف» for a 5-bedroom villa that starts at 1.889M); (2) when 2–3 projects fit it names the others in the same line; (3) a named project + «ارسل لي الوحدات» sends the matching units list right away (including a floor «above 15»), instead of the project card and a question; (4) it searches before asking for an area, so a request nothing matches gets an honest «ما عندنا» with the closest real option. (**Bulk project send.** A rep can multi-select several projects and send them to ONE client in order. Entry points: the in-chat Projects & Units browser (`ProjectsUnitsBrowser` «إرسال متعدد»), the Project Finder header («إرسال» over the selected projects), and the Projects list («إرسال متعدد» → pick a client via `ClientPicker`). `BulkProjectSendFlow` walks each project { compose message (shared `ProjectMessageComposeStep`, extracted from `ProjectWhatsAppFlow` so both flows share the saved-message/AI-rewrite/fact-check/language step) → pick files (`ProjectFilePickerModal preselect="bulk"`: the brochure document + the first 3 photos pre-checked — the "top 3" is hero + next two, order-based since there is no per-image quality metadata; the brochure is the document with `primary_category='brochure'` (or a «بروشور»/brochure name), and other documents/videos start unchecked) } → REVIEW (reorder/remove) → SEND ALL; nothing is sent until then. `enqueueBulkProjectSend` (`src/lib/projects/bulkProjectSend.ts`) then ENQUEUES the whole plan to the backend and returns immediately — the rep no longer waits for delivery. It computes a staggered delivery schedule and hands every message to the durable `scheduled_whatsapp_jobs` queue via the existing scheduled-send primitives (text → `sendChatMessage`/`startNewChat` with `deliverAt`; media → `sendProjectImageMessages` with `deliverAt` → `send-media-batch`), each of which returns as soon as the row is inserted. Per project: TEXT at `base`, media at `base + k·4s` (uniform ~4s cadence — send-media-batch takes a `staggerSeconds` override), the next project one tick after the previous project last media. **Cross-project ordering is now deliver_at staggering, not sent-gating** — the Fly worker drains `scheduled_whatsapp_claim_due` in deliver_at order, the SAME cadence a single-project scheduled gallery already uses; the modal shows a brief «في قائمة الإرسال / Queued to send» confirmation and closes. Every queued message is visible + cancelable in the conversation scheduled-message strip. Bulk media order = `orderSelectedRefsBulk` (documents→photos→videos). Durability: once queued, a message survives refresh/tab-close/worker-restart; only projects not yet enqueued when a tab closes mid-"Send all" (a ~1s window) are dropped.) (**Arabic/English language selector on WhatsApp sends.** The rep now picks the send language at send time, independent of the app UI language. In `SendUnitsPdfModal` (units-table PDF + single-unit sheet) a «لغة الملف» toggle chooses the PDF content language; the modal now takes language-parameterized props (`titleFor`/`subtitleFor`/`filenameFor`/`captionFor`/`buildFor`) and builds `buildUnitsTablePdf`/`buildUnitPdf` with the chosen `isAr` (blobs cached per language; the caption resets to the language default unless the rep edited it). The local Download buttons in `UnitDrawer`/`UnitsInventory` still follow the app language. No change to `unitsPdf.ts` or `sendPdfToChat.ts` — the builders were already bilingual. **Per-language re-resolution (2026-09-02 follow-up):** picking English while the app UI is Arabic now RE-RESOLVES the project + units in the chosen language (`resolveProjectView`/`resolveUnitView` with `isAr` + `getEntityFieldText`) instead of reusing the app-language views, so the PDF header project name (translation variant, e.g. "Sawari Mujabbab"), city/district (English geo `name_en`), the modal title/subtitle/caption, and every option chip render in the selected language. Conservative by design (harm is asymmetric — a false skip would send a stale price): any number it can't prove current — or a now-sold-out project whose saved text still quotes a price (QA-003) — still runs the real fact-check. **Send popups close instantly.** The «محادثة جديدة» / project-message popup (`StartChatModal` via `ProjectWhatsAppFlow` — finder, in-chat Projects & Units, Client Options, Suggested Projects) and the units-table / unit-sheet PDF popup (`SendUnitsPdfModal`) now close the MOMENT the rep clicks Send/Schedule. (2) Unpinning requires an actual UPWARD scroll (scrollTop decreased), not merely distance-from-bottom > 60px, so growth below the viewport can never unpin; any scroll ending within 60px of the bottom re-pins. It computes a staggered delivery schedule and hands every message to the durable `scheduled_whatsapp_jobs` queue via the existing scheduled-send primitives (text → `sendChatMessage`/`startNewChat` with `deliverAt`; media → `sendProjectImageMessages` with `deliverAt` → `send-media-batch`), each of which returns as soon as the row is inserted. Per project: TEXT at `base`, media at `base + k·4s` (uniform ~4s cadence — send-media-batch takes a `staggerSeconds` override), the next project one tick after the previous project last media. **Cross-project ordering is now deliver_at staggering, not sent-gating** — the Fly worker drains `scheduled_whatsapp_claim_due` in deliver_at order, the SAME cadence a single-project scheduled gallery already uses; the modal shows a brief «في قائمة الإرسال / Queued to send» confirmation and closes. The local Download buttons in `UnitDrawer`/`UnitsInventory` still follow the app language. The old full resync upserted ~380 records unconditionally on every Chats-page open (each firing the whole 29-trigger records stack + a Realtime event), and because WAHA has no per-chat status concept it kept RE-OPENING CRM-closed chats — the reason «مغلقة» stayed at 0 and the Done button appeared broken since the WAHA cutover; when the sync raced an empty store it also re-created every chat with no status/client_link (the 2026-08-24 inbox wipe, repaired from backups). New client-side builders `src/lib/projects/unitsPdf.ts` (html2canvas→jsPDF, JPEG-embedded so a 30-row table is ~0.3 MB not ~20 MB; can't-hang image readiness instead of `img.decode()`), send glue `src/lib/projects/sendPdfToChat.ts` (`uploadLocalFile` → `sendChatMessage` `kind:'document'`), and a confirm dialog `SendUnitsPdfModal`; wired through `UnitsInventory` + `UnitDrawer` (both take an optional `chatPdf` context — Send appears only in a chat, Download always) and `ProjectsUnitsBrowser` (now takes `chatWid`). When `client_link` was never persisted (or got reverted to null by a record refetch — the field is null in the DB for most chats), the header showed «إضافة كعميل» / «إضافة جهة اتصال جديدة» even for a number that clearly belongs to a saved client, and briefly flickered the client in whenever a relink sweep ran in memory. Both panes now resolve the client the same way they resolve contacts/advertisers: prefer a live stored `client_link` when it points at a live client, else fall back to a live phone match against the loaded clients — a pure function of (phone, loaded clients), so it can't be clobbered by a stored-field race. Everything downstream (client card link, preference chips, Client Options, WhatsApp follow-up completion, study card) now lights up from the live match too. `ChatDetail.tsx` (`linkedClient` now a `useMemo` with a `matchRecordByPhone` fallback), `ChatList.tsx` (`clientByChatId` live map behind `clientOf`). **Companion matcher fix:** `matchRecordByPhone` (`normalize.ts`) now also compares KSA-canonical forms via the new `ksaCanonicalPhone` (mirrors the SQL `ksa_phone_canon`), so a client stored in local `05…` form finally matches a `966…` chat — the raw digit-suffix compare missed it because the leading `0` breaks the suffix. The `contacts` model was simultaneously un-archived into its own top-level sidebar section and extended to name*/phone*/company/position/email/notes with phone as its duplicate-check field. Queued messages for the open chat render as gold chips above the composer input (delivery time + body preview + attachment marker) via the new `GET /api/haberchat/scheduled?deviceId&phone` proxy (Haberchat `GET /messages?status=queued&devices=…`, digit-normalized phone filter), each cancelable with one click (`DELETE /api/haberchat/scheduled?id=` → Haberchat `DELETE /messages/{id}`; only works while still queued — a failed cancel re-syncs the strip). (1) **Webhook auth is carrier-tolerant:** `api/webhook/haberchat.ts` now accepts the shared secret from ANY of header / `?secret=` / `?token=` — matching if any non-empty candidate equals the expected value — instead of the old header-first precedence where an empty/stale `X-Webhook-Secret` header shadowed the valid URL secret and 401'd the delivery (Haberchat retried each until a 200, so NO messages were lost — verified by per-chat count diff Haberchat-vs-DB; the 401s were the "failing webhook" emails). Rejects log carrier PRESENCE (never values) for forensics. (2) **`size ≥ 50` guard:** Haberchat now 400s `/sync` when `size < 50` (verified live: 49→400, 50→200); `api/_lib/haberchat.ts#listMessages` clamps up and the proxy's min is 50. (3) **Thread falls back to the DB mirror:** when the live Haberchat fetch fails, `loadMessagesForChat` now renders the thread from the `chat_messages` mirror (`loadMessagesFromMirror`, newest-first page mapped by the shared `dbRowToChatMessage`) instead of showing a false «لا توجد رسائل في هذه المحادثة بعد» empty state — the exact symptom users reported. Generation does two things at once: (a) **AI message text** — `POST /api/templates/listing-message` resolves the listing's structured facts server-side (unit type, district + city via the geo cascade, area, bedrooms, bathrooms) and hands them + the free-text Arabic `description` to Claude (force-tool), which writes an editable bilingual message that weaves in the real amenities (it's told to invent nothing); (b) **photo text-removal** — `POST /api/templates/clean-listing-images` creates the chat_templates DRAFT server-side and fans out ONE `generation_jobs` row (`kind='clean-text'`) per listing photo. The Fly worker (`runCleanTextJob.ts`, new `cleanTextPollLoop`) cleans each photo with **FLUX.2 [klein] 4B edit** (`fal-ai/flux-2/klein/4b/edit`, prompt-driven) — which reliably removes ALL overlays (banners, agency logos, the aqar.fm watermark, CTAs, social handles) per an explicit removal prompt while keeping the building, angle, lighting, and aspect, at ~3 s and ~$0.005/photo. The worker downloads each source photo itself and submits the bytes as a data URI — images.aqar.fm started blocking fal’s server-side fetcher (2026-07-18), so raw-URL submission fails with `file_download_error` for ANY model. (Model history, all live-tested on real Aqar photos: 2026-06-30 — the dedicated `image-editing/text-removal` model left big stylized banners; `nano-banana-pro/edit` REFUSES watermark removal as "unsafe content"; GPT Image 2 edit works but at ~3 min + ~$0.02–0.22/photo. 2026-07-18 — FLUX.2 klein 4B matched GPT Image 2 on overlay removal + photo fidelity in a same-photos side-by-side and replaced it; its one weakness is slightly uneven background fill on collage tiles. Model + prompt are env-overridable via `FAL_CLEAN_TEXT_MODEL_ID` / `FAL_CLEAN_TEXT_PROMPT`.) re-hosts to `marketing-assets` + a `media_assets` row, and fills the draft's `data.cleaning[i]`. Concurrency: every worker write to the draft uses `record_save` optimistic-concurrency + retry (image-chats v3 posture); the browser never writes the draft during cleaning (echo-dedup). Model: `FAL_CLEAN_TEXT_MODEL_ID` (env-swappable). (a) **Existing client** (default): an advanced client search in a new `ClientPicker.tsx` that REUSES the records-table search engine — `buildRecordSearchText` / `buildExpandedFieldSearchText` / `normalizeForSearch` over a memoized per-record index, with an "All fields / specific field" scope dropdown built from `collectViewFields(clientsModel)`, and the user's view-scope honoured through `useApplyViewScope`. Each result renders `client_code • client_name • phone_number`; the code/name/phone slugs are resolved from the model schema (first `auto_id` field, card-title field, first `phone` field), not hardcoded, so Builder renames don't break it. Picking a client auto-fills the phone (read-only) and links the new conversation to that client DIRECTLY — `startNewChat` gained an optional `clientRecordId` that, when still a live client, sets `client_link` outright; explicit selection beats the phone-match heuristic, which still runs for the manual path. (b) **Manual number**: reuses the shared `PhoneInput` (country-code dropdown + number, `splitPhone`/`joinPhone`); the two parts are combined to E.164 before sending and run through the existing `normalizePhone`. The composer gained an **Insert template** button that opens the existing `TemplatePickerModal`; the chosen template's text is inserted into the textarea — empty textarea → inserted directly, non-empty → appended below a blank line, never overwriting — and stays fully editable (free-text / template-only / template+edit all supported). Send is disabled until a valid recipient (normalized phone, or a selected client that has a phone) AND a non-empty trimmed message both exist. Text-only for new chats: a template's media attachment is NOT carried into the first message of a brand-new conversation — only its text is inserted. **Manual-entry client recognition (same day):** in Manual number mode, a typed number that matches an existing client surfaces a "Matches a saved client" chip — reusing `resolveClientLink` + the now-shared `resolveClientSlugs`/`recordToPickedClient` helpers exported from `ClientPicker.tsx`, scoped via `useApplyViewScope` — that flips to the locked client card on click. The match uses the same matcher `startNewChat` uses to auto-link, so the hint and the eventual `client_link` always agree. **AI Project Message templates (same day):** the chat_templates editor gained a **Generate with AI** action (`ProjectMessageGeneratorModal`) that turns selected `our_projects` records into bilingual (ar+en) WhatsApp message templates. It resolves the 9 message facts client-side (`src/lib/projectMessageFacts.ts`) — name / city / district / min-price / brochure / location from the linked `all_projects`; bedroom & bathroom ranges from the auto-calculated `our_projects` unit rollups; unit types as the distinct `unit_type`s across the project's units — then composes each message DETERMINISTICALLY (no AI/endpoint — `composeProjectMessage`): project name, then one labeled line per PRESENT field, no greeting/closing, exact labels (price = «الأسعار تبدأ من» / «Prices start from»); missing fields are OMITTED from the body, never written as «not available» (the preview still flags them). [Superseded same day: the original ship used an `/api/templates/generate-project-message` Haiku endpoint; replaced with deterministic composition per the user's exact-format spec + the live-slug/rollup resolution fix.] Bulk = one template per project, each reviewed (edit ar/en · regenerate · skip · save) before anything is written. Saved templates are `language:'both'` and link to the master via `data.project_id` (a new `lookup` field on chat_templates → all_projects, added by migration `2026-06-08_chat_templates_project_link.sql`). A `client_link` UUID that points at a since-deleted clients record is treated as unlinked, so a freshly-created client with the same phone number picks the chat up on the next sweep (boot, message arrival, Chats page mount, or clients save). `deleteRecord` still deliberately leaves dangling JSONB lookups (per its H8 comment) — the relinkers absorb the staleness instead. Past behavior, where a chat would silently stick to a dead UUID forever, is fixed. The panel reuses the same `MessageBubble` from the chats page (so the visual is identical), groups messages by day, and tags each bubble with a tiny "via &lt;our device name&gt;" label so the user can tell which of our numbers handled the conversation. **Previously (2026-07-22):** In-app client studies («توليد دراسة») — client-linked chats gain a button that queues an app-triggered Claude Code session (`claude_jobs` + `StudyJobCard` + `scripts/claude-study-runner.mjs`) producing a branded Arabic market-study PDF per the `/client-study` skill; the rep reviews the PDF + WhatsApp draft before sending.


## What it is (in plain English)
A two-pane WhatsApp inbox inside the Wassell app. The left pane is the list of every conversation synced from a connected WhatsApp number; the right pane is the open conversation — message history, an image/document composer, and a status + labels header. Staff can reply from the browser, start new chats to numbers they've never messaged, attach images and documents, mark conversations resolved or archived, and see each conversation auto-linked to the matching Client record. New inbound WhatsApp messages appear in the list and the open thread live — no refresh needed.

Chat traffic itself runs through **Haberchat** (`api.haber.chat/v1`), a WhatsApp Business gateway. The app never talks to Haberchat directly — a set of Vercel serverless functions in `api/haberchat/*` proxy every call server-side so the Haberchat API token stays off the client. A webhook handler receives every inbound message + ack update, writes them to Supabase, and Supabase Realtime pushes them to every open browser tab.

## Why it exists
Real estate sales pipelines in Saudi run on WhatsApp. Before this module, staff replied from their personal phones and copy-pasted summaries back into the CRM. Now every conversation lives next to the matching Client record, is searchable, is assignable, and becomes a first-class record the rest of the app (workflows, dashboards, reporting) can act on.

## Key behaviors
- **AI activity cards instead of the composer (2026-10-05).** For a client-linked chat whose AI switch is on (not paused, not a group / operations line), `ChatDetail` renders `AiActivityPanel` where the composer was: a horizontal row of cards — summary · what it understood (the last turn's reading, plus the client's saved preferences — the same chips as the header, which the agent reads before every reply; so the card is never empty for a client with a profile) · searches · what it found · what it did · what it changed (the `AiChangesSection` list with Undo, `bare`) · run log. Data from `GET /api/chat-ai-activity?clientId&chatWid` (service role behind `assertCanAccessRecord`), which reads `wa_agent_runs` (one row per agent turn: reading, searches with their top 5, actions, reply, checker problems, tool trace — written by `api/_lib/salesAgent/runLog.ts` from `turn.ts` for all three run kinds; a failed insert is logged and never fails the turn), message counts from `chat_messages` (`send_source='ai'` = AI), `client_ai_changes`, `chat_outcome_suggestions`, `portal_registration_jobs` / `client_portal_registrations`, `ai_actions`, `wa_agent_questions`, `ai_notifications`, appointments / visits and `v_client_project_interest`. The panel re-loads when the thread grows. It folds away with a chevron button at the end of its bar — the same button the chat's top section uses — leaving one line with counts (the title also toggles); the choice is remembered per browser. «اكتب رداً» shows the composer for that chat only (switching chats returns to the cards); a link above the composer returns to the cards.

- **One chat per number + number switcher (new 2026-10-04).** A contact who talked to more than one of our WhatsApp numbers appears as a SEPARATE chat per number — its own row, preview, time, unread badge and thread — and whatever is sent while one of them is open (text, attachments, project photos, PDFs) leaves from THAT number. When more than one number is active, a row of chips sits above the search box: «كل الأرقام» / All numbers (every per-number chat), then each active number — the default as «المبيعات» / Sales, the operations line as «العمليات» / Operations, any other by its display name (e.g. «طلبات العملاء» / Client requests) — each with its chat count. Old sessions of the sales phone (`wassel_main`, Haberchat ids) count as the Sales chat. Opening a per-number chat clears only that number's unread. The chat header's «آخر رسالة» / Last time is that per-number chat's own last message, not the contact's latest on any number. **How:** the conversation record stays ONE per contact (its id is uuidv5 of the wid, which the send check, AI gate, send worker and read tracking all depend on). `data.lines` lists every number it has messages on and `data.line_meta[<number>]` holds that number's last message time / preview / direction and unread count — written by the webhook on every message (`api/_lib/chatIngest.ts` `withLine` / `withLineMeta`) and back-filled by `supabase/migrations/2026-10-04_chat_lines_backfill.sql` + `2026-10-04_chat_line_meta_backfill.sql`. The list builds rows with `chatLineRows` (`src/pages/Chats/lib/chatLines.ts`); a row opens `/model/chats/<id>?line=<number>`, the thread pages only that number's `chat_messages` (by `device_id`), and the open number is registered in `src/lib/chat/openLine.ts` so `sendChatMessage` and the project-photo sender send from it.
- **«اقتراحات الذكاء الاصطناعي» / "AI suggestions" — one AI card with tabs (2026-09-29).** The preferences card below was renamed (Sparkles icon; collapsed bar «اقتراحات الذكاء الاصطناعي») because it will hold more than preferences. Its header carries the title, the voice-note badge, a segmented control with two tabs — **«تفضيلات العميل»** (badge = preference slides still to act on) and **«النتائج»** (badge = 1 when the AI has a live suggested outcome for the current follow-up) — and the collapse chevron. The preferences tab is exactly the slider described in the next bullets (its pills, ‹ › and «١ / ٤» belong to that tab; switching tabs and back keeps the slide that was in view). The card opens on the first tab with a non-zero badge (preferences when both are empty) once both have loaded; after that the rep's choice stands for this chat until the card is unmounted. The «النتائج» tab is one slide-style card: «النتيجة المقترحة» + the outcome + a confidence chip («٨٥٪»), «للمهمة: …» (the task it applies to), the reasoning on one line with «المزيد» when it is cut off, the customer's words on one muted line, and «تأكيد النتيجة» / «اختيار نتيجة أخرى» / «تجاهل» — the same handlers as the task bar had (see "Task bar"). With no live suggestion it says «لا توجد نتيجة مقترحة الآن» and, when there is an open task, offers «تسجيل النتيجة». The collapsed bar's dot lights when either tab has something. The suggestion is loaded ONCE per chat (`useChatOutcomeSuggestion` in `ChatDetail`: one fetch, one realtime subscription, shared with the task bar).
- **«تفضيلات العميل» — the geography confirm card (2026-09-27, client-linked chats only).** Mounted in `ChatDetail` right under the study card and hidden with it when the header is collapsed; collapsible on its own (global preference `wassell_geo_pref_card_collapsed`). On open it loads the card for THIS chat (`GET /api/geo-preference/chat-card`). Never read → «لم تُقرأ هذه المحادثة بعد» + «اقرأ المواقع» (reads the chat with the geography AI — up to a minute, spinner «أقرأ المحادثة…», real error toasted on failure). Read → two short groups, «يريد» and «لا يريد», one line per place the customer mentioned, in the same words the geography grader uses («حدّد: حي النخيل (الرياض)», «حدّد: شمال / الرياض — 37 حيًا»), each with a checkbox ticked by default; an amber line under a place the second-opinion AI doubts (e.g. «المرسلات ذُكرت من المندوب … وليست تفضيلًا ذكره العميل»), and one muted line «قد يكون العميل ذكر أيضًا: …» for places it thinks were missed. A place the AI could not pin to a real location is shown with «لن يُحفظ» and cannot be ticked. «إظهار الخريطة» draws the ticked lines on the boundary map. **«احفظ في ملف العميل»** writes the ticked lines to the client's `location_items` through `/api/geo-preference/review` (all ticked = `confirm`, some unticked = `edit` with those places removed; merged onto what the client already has, never wiping it) → «✓ حُفظت في تفضيلات العميل». **«تجاهل»** = reject → «تم تجاهل القراءة» + «أعد القراءة». When the customer has written since the reading: «رسائل جديدة منذ آخر قراءة» + «حدّث». If another rep resolved the same reading first, the card says so and reloads. Any rep who can see the client can use it (not admin-only). The card never sends a message and never writes the client record except through its Save taps.
- **The card's layout — a slider of topic cards (2026-09-29).** Reps found the stacked sections a wall of text on top of the chat, so the card is now ONE row of tabs plus ONE short slide at a time. **Slides** exist only for what the rep can still act on, in this order: «الأماكن · المحادثة» (the chat's open reading, pending or must-confirm, with at least one place), «المواصفات · المحادثة» (the chat's pending preferences), then per call, newest first, «الأماكن · مكالمة ٣١/٨» and «المواصفات · مكالمة ٣١/٨». Saved, dismissed, replaced or empty readings are not slides (built by the pure `buildCardSlides`). **The strip** (wraps on a narrow screen): card title, voice-note badge, one pill per slide (icon, short label, item count; the active pill is filled copper), ‹ › and «١ / ٤», the re-read button where it showed before, the collapse chevron. With no slides the strip says «لا جديد · آخر قراءة …» (or «رسائل جديدة منذ آخر قراءة») + «أعد القراءة», or «لم تُقرأ هذه المحادثة بعد» + «اقرأ المواقع». **The slider** is a horizontal scroll-snap row, so swiping works on touch; pills and arrows jump to a slide and the active slide is tracked by what is on screen (works the same in Arabic and English). Each slide is capped at ~260 px with its own scroll. **Inside a slide**, items are small tiles (two columns on wider screens) — tap anywhere on a tile to tick / untick. A place tile shows the PLACE short and bold («الربوة», «جنوب الرياض · ٢٠ حيًا», «غرب طريق الملك فهد · ٥ كم», «قرب الرياض بارك · ٣ كم», «النرجس — الجزء الشمالي من طريق الملك سلمان»; `placementTitle` — a road side and a distance rule always show their side and distance, 2026-10-03) with a «يريد» / «لا يريد» chip, then the customer's words on one truncated line (the full placement sentence and quote are in the tooltip); a place the AI could not pin is muted, has no box and says «يحتاج تأكيد»; a district clipped to a side of a road that has NO part on that side (or whose part was never computed) is also muted with no box and says «لا جزء على هذا الجانب» / «تعذّر حساب الجزء» — saving it would have saved the whole district (`blockedReason` in `geoRows.ts`). A spec tile shows the field, the value, then the quote, with «مطابق للمحفوظ» or «سُجّلت بعد المكالمة» as a chip (the saved value and add-vs-replace rule are in the tooltip). The second-opinion AI's doubt is a small amber «مشكوك» chip; tapping it opens the reason under the tile without changing the tick. **Each slide's footer**: «احفظ المحدد (٣)», «تجاهل», and an ⓘ whose tooltip gives the save rule (added to saved places / multi-choice added, ranges replaced / fills empty fields only); the chat places slide also has the map toggle (the map opens under the slider while that slide is active); a call's places slide keeps save off with «للعميل أماكن محفوظة — لن نستبدلها» when the client already has places. After a save or dismiss the slide stays in place as a compact «✓ حُفظت» / «تم التجاهل» (plus «تُخطيت لأنها سُجّلت: …» for a call) until the chat is re-read or reopened. The collapsed bar is unchanged; its dot shows when any slide exists. Every request, 409 path and tick rule described below is unchanged.
- **The card reads by itself — the chat auto-read (2026-09-27).** A per-minute cron (`/api/cron/chat-auto-read`) reads every client-linked chat with unread customer text once the burst settles (90 s after the newest message, never more than 10 min after the oldest, held while a voice note is still being transcribed), and the card itself reads on open (trigger `open`) when the customer has written something unread. A batch that is only pleasantries («تمام», «👍», «السلام عليكم») is skipped by a free keyword gate — no model runs, the messages are just marked read. «اقرأ المواقع» / «أعد القراءة» still force a read that bypasses the gate. One reader per chat at a time (a lease). See [geo-preference-ability.md](geo-preference-ability.md) for the full timing rule.
- **«تفضيلات العميل من المحادثة» — preferences read from the same conversation (2026-09-27).** Under the places, the card lists the preference agent's suggestions for budget, unit type, area, bedrooms, purpose and amenities: the field name (from the live clients schema), the proposed value (option labels, formatted ranges), the customer's own words «…», the value saved on the client now (or «لا قيمة محفوظة»), and whether a save ADDS to the saved values (unit type / purpose / amenities) or REPLACES them (budget / area / bedrooms). Each line has a checkbox, ticked by default — except a line that would change nothing, which starts unticked with «مطابق للمحفوظ». **«احفظ المحدد في ملف العميل»** writes only the ticked lines (`POST /api/client-prefs/review` action `save`); **«تجاهل»** dismisses (`dismiss`, never touches the client). A 409 (someone else resolved it, or a newer reading replaced it) reloads the card. When the geography reading worked but the preference reading failed, the card says «قُرئت المواقع فقط — تعذّر استخراج التفضيلات».
- **«من مكالمة … — قالها العميل ولم تُسجَّل» — the call audit (2026-09-29).** Calls are NOT read for a live card (the rep logs during and after the call); instead each finished Hatif call is AUDITED once, for what the customer said that the salesperson forgot to put on the client. The auto-read cron (after its chat reads, within its time budget) picks calls longer than 20 s with a speaker-labelled transcript, at least 1 hour after hang-up, linked to a client (last 60 days), and runs the preference extractor on the call. Only the fields that are EMPTY on the client become a proposal; a field the client already has — same value or not — is never shown. Calls whose speakers could not be told apart are skipped (the extractor can't separate the salesperson's summary from the customer's words); places are read too, but only for a client with NO saved places (see the next paragraph). The card lists each call's proposal (pending, or decided in the last 24 h so the result shows once), newest first, just above the chat's own preference section: the field, the value, and the customer's quote «…». A field that someone filled since the call is unticked, disabled and marked «سُجّلت بعد المكالمة». **«أضف المحدد إلى ملف العميل»** saves fill-empty-only: the server re-checks each ticked field on the fresh client row and skips (never writes) any that is no longer empty — the result says «أُضيفت إلى ملف العميل» and, if any, «تُخطيت لأنها سُجّلت: …». «تجاهل» dismisses and never writes. The collapsed card's dot lights for a pending call proposal too.
- **«الأماكن» — places from a call (2026-09-29, call audit part 2).** The same audit also reads where the customer wants to buy from the call, through the chat card's own geography pipeline (a first read extracts; a call already read — e.g. by the calibration runs — is re-reviewed without a new extraction, so graded data is never re-extracted). It runs ONLY for a client whose places (`location_items`) are empty; a client with places is skipped. A place the SALESPERSON said («عندنا مشروع في النرجس») never shows, even when the model labelled it as the customer's: on a call a place counts only if the customer's own words contain it. The call's block in the card gains a «الأماكن» part: «يريد» / «لا يريد» lines exactly like the chat's own places, each with a tick box, then **«احفظ الأماكن في ملف العميل»** and «تجاهل». Saving is fill-empty-only: when the client has places by then the button is disabled and the card says «للعميل أماكن محفوظة — لن نستبدلها»; if places appeared after the card loaded the server refuses the save (409 `client_has_places`), nothing is written, and the proposal stays open for the rep to dismiss. Only proposals the audit itself made are listed (pending, or decided in the last 24 h) — the older calibration readings on the same calls never appear. A call with both preferences and places shows them in one block; the collapsed dot lights for pending places too.
- **Voice notes on the card (2026-09-27).** A transcribed inbound voice note is read as the customer's own words. An amber mic badge in the card header counts voice notes since the last reading that could NOT be transcribed (a rep has to listen); «جاري تفريغ رسالة صوتية…» shows while one is still being transcribed.
- **Intermittent send failures on iPhone fixed (2026-08-20).** Reps on the installed iOS app hit "it takes a couple of tries to send, with an error about a stolen key". Root cause: the Supabase client used the default `navigatorLock` (Web Locks API) to coordinate auth-token refresh; on iOS/Safari PWAs that lock gets held across app suspend/resume and then "stolen by another request", which stalls `getSession()` and the token refresh — so a send goes out slowly and sometimes without a fresh token, fails, and only works on retry. `src/lib/supabase.ts` now configures `auth: { lock: processLock }` (Supabase's in-memory lock, its recommended fix for this exact Safari/iOS symptom); `@supabase/auth-js` is pinned in `package.json` to make the import explicit.
- **Sending never blocks typing (fix 2026-08-18).** Pressing Send hands the message to the BACKGROUND and clears the box immediately — the textarea stays enabled and focused, so the rep can type and send the next message while the previous one is still in flight. There is no global "sending" flag any more: progress is **per message**, shown on that message's own optimistic bubble in the thread (clock → single tick → delivered/read, or a red **«لم تُرسل» / "Not sent"** state). Every send is bound to the conversation identity captured at the moment it was dispatched, so an in-flight send can never land in a chat the rep has since switched to. Attachments still upload before their message goes out (that is inherent), but the composer is free the whole time.
- **A failed send is loud, and never eats the text (fix 2026-08-18).** A message whose send fails keeps its bubble in the thread, outlined red, labelled «لم تُرسل» / "Not sent", with a **«إعادة المحاولة» / Retry** button that re-sends the identical payload (`retryChatMessage` — it drops the failed bubble and dispatches a fresh one with a new idempotency key; if the retry itself cannot produce a bubble, the original is put back, so Retry can neither lose nor duplicate the message). Failures that happen BEFORE any bubble exists — an attachment upload that never reached the send, a scheduled send the queue rejected — are toasted AND parked in a red strip above the composer with a **«استعادة» / Restore** button that puts the text and attachments back in the box (appended below anything typed since, never replacing it).
- **The composer only exists once the conversation identity is RESOLVED (fix 2026-08-18).** A conversation needs four things before it can be sent on — the record, its `wid`, its recipient `phone`, and a send-from device — and they arrive at different times (`wid`/`phone` when the chats list or the inbound webhook fills the record; the device overlay from a separate fire-and-forget load at boot). `resolveConversationIdentity` (`src/pages/Chats/lib/conversationIdentity.ts`) collapses them into ONE value: `ready` / `loading` / `unsupported` (group or channel) / `incomplete` (`missing-wid` | `missing-phone` | `no-device`). `ChatDetail` mounts `Composer` **only** for `ready` and passes the resolved identity by value; every other state renders a short bilingual notice instead (spinner + «جارٍ تحميل المحادثة…» while loading, an explicit reason otherwise). It is gated on identity ALONE — never on a previous send being in flight. **The bug this fixes:** the composer was enabled as soon as the record existed, so on a freshly-created or webhook-created chat a rep could type and send inside the resolution window; `sendChatMessage` then threw its identity error BEFORE creating the optimistic bubble, the composer had already cleared itself, and for a plain-text send nothing at all was toasted — the message simply vanished. `sendChatMessage` now returns those failures instead of throwing early: the bubble is created first, then marked failed with a toast, so even a race that slips through is visible and retryable.
- **Sidebar entry** `المحادثات / Chats` is a top-level item (not grouped) driven by a system model with `name: 'chats'`. The record-list dispatcher renders the split layout instead of the generic table view.
- **Split layout always on desktop.** Left pane (~360px) = conversation list. Right pane = detail or a "Select a conversation" placeholder. On mobile, whichever pane matches the URL takes the full width.
- **Native mobile experience (redesign 2026-08-18).** Below `md`, Chats behaves like a phone messaging app rather than a shrunk desktop page. **Container:** `ChatsSplitPage` goes edge-to-edge full-screen — it cancels the AppLayout `<main>` `px-4 py-6` with negative margins (`-mx-4 -mt-6`), drops the `rounded-2xl border` frame (both `md:`-gated), and is `h-[calc(100dvh-4.25rem)]` so it fills the viewport under the ~68px sticky app header with no top gap (measured live: the list starts at y≈70). **Conversation header** (`ChatDetail`) is a compact messaging bar: back · avatar · name · status pill · phone, plus a trailing cluster of an optional `tel:` **call** button, a **CRM actions** grid button (opens a bottom sheet), and a **⋯ overflow** menu. The old always-visible «إنهاء المحادثة» button and the inline 5–7-button CRM stack are gone from the mobile bar: End/Reopen/Archive live in the ⋯ menu; the CRM actions (Projects & Units, Linked client, Client options, Log interaction, hand-to-AI, or Add-as-client/Add-contact) live in the sheet. Preference chips are a single horizontal-scroll row; kind/last-message meta is hidden. **Thread + composer** (`MessageThread`, `Composer`) drop their `.card` frame below `md`, so the thread is a full-bleed scroll area (message history ≈75–80% of the screen) and the composer sits edge-to-edge pinned at the bottom. **Everything above `md` is unchanged** — all edits are `md:`-gated, and the CRM actions render from one shared `CrmActions` component (inline on desktop, sheet on mobile) so behaviour never diverges.
- **Opening a record from the chat is a POPUP, never a page — MOBILE ONLY (2026-08-19; scoped to `< md` on 2026-08-20).** On a phone (`useIsMobile()`, `< 768px`) every CRM action stays inside the conversation as an overlay. **On desktop the original behaviour is kept unchanged**: the client profile and «عميل مرتبط» open in a NEW TAB, Contact / Advertiser `navigate` to their record page, Client-Options / finder "View source" open a new tab, and the finder map uses its default (`auto`) gesture handling. All the popup/overlay code below is reached only when `useIsMobile()` is true (the triggers branch on it; the overlays never mount on desktop). Code-splitting and the mobile layout redesign are the only chat changes that also apply on the laptop.
  - **Client profile** (header avatar / name / «عميل مرتبط») opens the full **360 client cockpit** (`ClientDetailPage`) as a full-screen overlay over the chat — the purpose-built page (overview / preferences / options / timeline / WhatsApp / calls / notes), not the generic form. `ClientDetailPage` gained optional `clientId` + `onClose` props (all embedded behaviour gated on `onClose`, so the `/model/clients/:id` route is byte-identical); `DetailHeader` gained an optional `onBack` so its Back button closes the overlay. Advanced-view and Find-more inside the overlay deliberately hand off to the full route.
  - A matched **Contact** / **Advertiser** / **Project officer** (no bespoke page) opens `RecordFormModal` — the record's fields in an overlay with an **«فتح الصفحة الكاملة» / Open full page** escape hatch.
  - **Projects & Units** now works from the mobile action sheet: its browser overlay was moved OUT of the sheet up to `ChatDetail` (previously the sheet-close unmounted the button before its drawer could open — the "nothing happens" bug). Same for the client 360 overlay — both live at chat level so dismissing the sheet can't kill them.
  - Client options + the embedded Project Finder, Log interaction, and the intake flows were already overlays. The finder's header action row gained `flex-wrap`, and its **map now pans with one finger** on touch (`gestureHandling:'greedy'` — the default `cooperative` demanded two fingers and read as a broken map inside the modal).
  - **Drilling into an option's source stays inside the popup (2026-08-19).** In the Client Options popup, an option card's **«عرض المصدر» / View source** used to open the project/unit/listing in a NEW TAB (`target="_blank"`), stranding a mobile rep with no way back. It now opens the source as an overlay *inside* the popup whose Back / close returns to the options list — same in-place pattern as the popup's Finder/Browse modes. A **project** source opens the **rich Project detail page** (`ProjectDetailPage`, parameterized with optional `recordId`/`modelName`/`onClose`, all gated so the `/model/all_projects/:id` route is byte-identical — its Exit becomes a «رجوع / Back» to Client Options); a **unit / market listing** opens `RecordFormModal` (with an «Open full page» hand-off). `RecordFormModal` now on-demand-fetches SUMMARY models (market_listings) from `unified_records` by id — they live in a slim slice outside the main `records` store, so without this the modal rendered an EMPTY form for a market-listing source. `ClientOptionsTab` gained an optional `onOpenSource` callback (absent = the old new-tab link, kept for the full-page 360 on desktop); the project overlay sits at `z-40` so its own edit-master popup stacks above it. **The embedded Project Finder does the same** — `SuggestedProjectsView`'s "Details" (`onOpenDetails`) used `window.open('_blank')`, so a project opened there had an Exit that went to the All Projects list, not back to the finder; it now takes the same `onOpenSource` callback and opens the source **stacked above** the popup (the finder stays mounted at `z-30`, results intact), so Back returns to the finder exactly where the rep left it.
- **List rows** sort by `last_message_at` desc. Each row shows avatar (first letter, copper for unread), name (or phone), last message preview (truncated), relative timestamp (time / Yesterday / short date), unread pill, status badge, and a device label when >1 number is active. **Client-linked rows additionally show preference chips** (new 2026-07-02): unit type(s), area range, and a single city · district · element location chip resolved from the linked client's preferences (see below).
- **Tabs: All / Clients / Advertisers / Other (new 2026-07-02; Advertisers added later the same day; Advertisers tab OFF since 2026-09-27 — model archived, D47, so it renders only while `advertisers` is not archived).** A segmented row under the search box splits the list by counterpart: **Clients** = conversations whose `client_link` points at a live clients record; **Advertisers («المعلنون»)** = conversations whose phone matches an `advertisers` record (computed live by the shared digits-suffix phone matcher — nothing stored on the chat, so REGA-lookup-created advertisers link instantly and deletions self-heal; a chat can be in both Clients and Advertisers); **Other** = strictly neither (developers, offices, unknown numbers). Counts on each tab track the current search. Advertiser-matched rows show a gold «معلن · name» chip, and the search also matches the advertiser's name. On the Clients tab a second filter row appears: **«مفتوحة» Open (default) / «بانتظار الرد» Waiting for reply / «مغلقة» Closed** (Waiting added 2026-07-21), with counts — **Waiting** = the linked client has a not-done WhatsApp follow-up parked in `message_sent_waiting_response` (derived live from the followups store, nothing stored on the chat; the chat pops back to Open the moment the inbound reconciler flips the task); **Open** = not closed AND not waiting (i.e. needs us); **Closed** = status `resolved` or `archived` (the Done button was clicked, or it was archived). The chosen tab/filter survives opening a chat (module-scope persistence — the two chats routes remount the list component). The list search also matches the linked client's name. *(The original same-day Interested / Needs reply / Unread filter chips were replaced by Open/Closed at the user's request; the `isInterestedClient` / `isAwaitingReply` helpers and the `last_message_flow` stamping remain available.)*
- **Done / Reopen button (new 2026-07-02).** The detail header carries a prominent one-click **«إنهاء المحادثة» / Done** button (copper, `CheckCheck` icon): when the rep finishes a conversation, one click sets status `resolved` — optimistic via the same `patchChat` → Haberchat PATCH path as the status pill (toast + revert on failure) — and the chat immediately moves from the Clients tab's Open list to Closed. On a closed chat the button becomes a subtle **«إعادة فتح» / Reopen** (→ `active`). The 3-way status pill (active/resolved/archived) still exists next to the name for finer control. *(Mobile, 2026-08-18: the standalone Done button is no longer permanently in the header — End/Reopen/Archive moved into the ⋯ overflow menu; the same `requestStatusChange` path and follow-up-completion popup are reused. Desktop keeps the inline button.)*
- **WhatsApp activity bridge (new 2026-07-21).** On every ingested message of a **client-linked** chat, the webhook (both the WAHA `chatIngest` path and the legacy Haberchat handler) calls a SECURITY DEFINER DB reconciler keyed on message direction: **outbound** arms/creates the client's WhatsApp follow-up as waiting-for-customer (+24h escalation deadline) and cancels open booking / no-show-recovery call tasks (`cancel_reason='contacted_via_whatsapp'`); **inbound** runs the reply reconciliation and creates a "your turn" WhatsApp task when the client has no open follow-up at all. Best-effort — a reconciler failure never fails the webhook (the message + chat bump are already persisted; the next message retries). Terminal-stage clients (خاسر / مغلق ناجح / غير مؤهل) are skipped. This is what keeps the Waiting filter here, the My Tasks Waiting tab, and the escalation machinery honest against the real conversation. Full semantics: [sales-process.md](sales-process.md).
- **Ad-sourced leads auto-onboard (new 2026-08-23).** An opening inbound message that resolves to one of our paid Click-to-WhatsApp ads (`chat_messages.meta.ad.resolved`) is treated as a declared lead. BEFORE the conversation is bumped, the webhook calls `mos_capture_ad_acquisition` (SECURITY DEFINER), which **finds-or-creates the client by canonical phone** — a new client takes its name from the WhatsApp push name (or the number), stage «جديد», source «ترويج» (Promotion) and `creation_source='ad_inbound'`; an existing client simply gains «ترويج» in its sources — and appends one immutable **`client_attributions`** ledger row tying the client to the ad (idempotent: one row per client+ad; first-touch when the client had no prior touch, else latest-touch). Because the client is created + linked first, the ordinary inbound reconciler (above) then spins up the "your turn" WhatsApp follow-up with **no rep approval**. This intentionally BYPASSES the manual «إضافة كعميل» intake door for ad leads only — clicking a paid ad is a strong enough signal to start the sales process automatically; every other unknown number still uses the two-door filing above. Fire-safe (a capture failure is logged, never blocks ingest) and gated on the message being genuinely new. See [marketing-workspace.md](marketing-workspace.md) for the ledger + the acquisition read surface, and [clients.md](clients.md) for the client-form «كيف وصلنا هذا العميل» panel.
  - **Ad leads without an ad ID (2026-09-28).** WhatsApp sometimes marks the opening message as coming from our ad (`entryPointConversionSource: 'ctwa_ad'` and/or `conversionSource: 'FB_Ads'`) but omits the `externalAdReply` card that carries the ad ID. `extractAdReferral` (`api/_lib/waha.ts`) keeps that mark as `meta.ad` with `ad_id: null`, `ad_id_missing: true`, the entry point, the app and the opaque `ctwa_payload`. `attachAdResolution` then infers the campaign from the greeting the ad's WhatsApp button sends — «مهتم بمشروع X», one line — via **`mos_infer_ad_from_greeting`**: only campaigns with a running ad, a running execution and a live campaign are candidates; the name matches the project name, or its part before « - » (the older «مينا 52» button for «مينا 52 - النرجس»). One campaign → `resolved` carries it with every ad field null and `inferred: true`; none, or two → `resolved` stays null. Either way the webhook calls `mos_capture_ad_acquisition`, so the lead is **onboarded like any ad lead** (client, «ترويج», follow-up); the ledger row (source **`inferred`**, no ad) is written only when a campaign was inferred, and that row makes the client a portal auto-registration candidate. It is **never credited to a single ad** — per-ad counts read `resolved.ad_id`. If WhatsApp later delivers a TAGGED copy of the same message (it can send both, the same second), the tagged row **supersedes** the inferred one (same execution/campaign, within 30 minutes), keeping its first/latest position. Rare: 1 in ~160 ad greetings between 16 Aug and 28 Sep. The webhook logs every such lead (`ad lead without an ad ID`).
- **Filing an unlinked chat — two doors (contact door added 2026-08-10).** An unlinked chat's header now offers **both** ways to file the number, because not every unknown number is a prospect: **«إضافة كعميل» / "Add as a client"** (the lead-intake flow below) and **«إضافة جهة اتصال جديدة» / "Add a new contact"** (`ContactIntakeModal`). The contact door saves a `contacts` record with name + phone prefilled from the chat (plus optional company / position / notes) and deliberately creates **no client, no follow-up task, and no sales-process state** — an address-book entry, not a lead. It warns in-modal when a contact with that number already exists, and hides itself once one does. The chat is tied back by **live phone matching** exactly like advertisers (nothing is written onto the chat record), and unlinks on its own if the contact is deleted. **The name WE filed the number under becomes the conversation's display name** (`resolveChatDisplayName`: linked client → saved contact → WhatsApp push name → phone → «—»), in the list row title, the thread header, and the avatar letter. `data.name` is only the PUSH NAME — whatever the person set on their own WhatsApp profile, often nothing at all — so before this a client saved as ناصر التويجري still showed as `+966538882982` and one saved as سلطان showed as "Sultan".
