Client wins over contact because the sales relationship is the stronger one and the two rarely coexist on one number. The **«عميل مرتبط» / "Linked client"** and **«جهة اتصال» / "Contact"** pills (each click → its record) and the list-row contact chip then only mark *what kind* of number it is, without repeating the name; the identifying phone stays under the title in both panes. A matched contact does **not** move the chat out of the **Other** tab — it is still neither a client nor an advertiser; the chip just says the number is already known and whose it is. Contact names are searchable from the list's search box. (The lead-intake button was relabelled from «عميل محتمل؟ إنشاء وبدء المتابعة» to the shorter «إضافة كعميل» so the two doors read as a matched pair; its behavior is unchanged.)
- **Lead intake from an unlinked chat (new 2026-07-21).** When a chat has no linked client, the header offers **«إضافة كعميل» / "Add as a client"** (`LeadIntakeModal`): step 1 creates the client with name + phone prefilled from the chat (stage جديد; the existing First-Follow-up workflow creates the booking-call task and the relink sweep attaches the chat); step 2 asks how the follow-up should start — **Call now** pulls the workflow-created booking call's `scheduled_datetime` to NOW (top of the rep's Actions list as a hot lead; if the workflow didn't produce one, the task is created so the lead can't fall through), or **Keep in WhatsApp** cancels the booking call and creates a WhatsApp task in the "your turn" state (`whatsapp_state='replied'` — they wrote first, we owe the reply).
- **Header actions of a linked chat (reshaped 2026-09-27).** The pills are: «المشاريع والوحدات», **«ملف العميل» / "Client profile"** (was «عميل مرتبط» — opens the client record), «خيارات العميل», **«أدوات» / "Tools"**, «إشعار المسؤول» and «تسجيل في البوابة». «أدوات» opens a small pop-up (`ChatToolsMenu`, portaled + fixed-positioned so the header never clips it; closes on outside click, Escape, scroll or a choice) holding the three less-frequent actions: «تسجيل تواصل», «حجز موعد», «تسجيل زيارة» — same modals as before. The «تسليم للمساعد الذكي» / "Hand to AI" pill is gone; the toggle only renders on a chat the AI agent ALREADY manages («المساعد يدير المحادثة — إيقاف») so a rep can always take it back. Same set in the mobile bottom sheet. The bullets below describe each action; where they say "beside" another pill, read "inside «أدوات»".
- **«طلب بحث مفتوح» pill (new 2026-09-28).** When the chat's linked client has an open unanswered request (any status other than option-found / client-dropped), an amber «طلب بحث مفتوح» / "Open search request" pill sits right after «ملف العميل» in the header, so nobody pitches stock we already know does not fit while a search runs. Clicking it opens the Sales Workspace «الطلبات غير المجابة» tab. It reads the requests already loaded in the store, so it only shows for users who can see that request. See [unanswered-requests.md](unanswered-requests.md). Replies from real-estate offices we messaged about a request land in this inbox like any chat (under «أخرى» / Other) and are also stamped onto the request by a database trigger.
- **Log interaction on a linked chat (new 2026-07-21).** The header of a client-linked chat offers **«تسجيل تواصل» / "Log interaction"** (`LogInteractionModal`) for contacts that happen outside any task — the rep called, the client called in, a WhatsApp exchange, a walk-in. The rep picks the direction, an outcome (from the sales-process catalog for the relevant follow-up type — outcomes requiring an existing appointment/visit record are excluded; those need the full Workspace), notes, and any outcome-required extra fields (validated with the same bilingual field labels as the Workspace). By default it **completes the client's current open task** with that outcome (overridable: keep it open, or cancel it); when there is no task (or it's kept/cancelled) it **creates a follow-up and completes it** — create-then-update via `saveRecord`, so the normal outcome workflows fire and move the client exactly like the Workspace. The chat record id is stamped as completion evidence (`completed_by_chat_id`).
- **Register in portal (new 2026-09-14).** Beside «إشعار المسؤول» the CRM-actions cluster of a client-linked chat carries **«تسجيل في البوابة» / "Register in portal"**: it opens `RegisterLeadPortalModal`, which registers the linked client in the broker portal behind a project (developer / marketer / officer) using a Browserbase browser on the Fly worker, asks the rep for the portal's OTP mid-run, and shows the screenshots as proof. Full PRD: `lead-portal-registration.md` (#37); portal recipes: `docs/lead-portal-recipes.md`.
- **Book appointment / Record a visit from a linked chat (new 2026-08-31).** The CRM-actions cluster of a client-linked chat carries two more buttons — **«حجز موعد» / "Book appointment"** and **«تسجيل زيارة» / "Record a visit"** — beside «تسجيل تواصل». They reuse the Follow-up Workspace's own `QuickAppointmentModal` / `QuickVisitModal` (project + date/time for an appointment; project + date + result for a visit), prefilled with the linked client, phone and the current user as sales rep, and create an `appointments` / `visits` record via the normal `saveRecord` path — so the appointment and Visit→After-Visit workflows fire exactly as they do from the Workspace. Both modals accept a nullable `followupId`: when the chat's client has an active WhatsApp follow-up it is stamped as `source_followup_id`, otherwise the record is created with no source follow-up. The buttons appear only for a linked client (an unlinked chat shows the intake doors instead), inline on desktop and inside the mobile CRM bottom sheet via the shared `CrmActions` component. This closes the gap that made appointment/visit outcomes Workspace-only — those still can't be *logged as a task outcome* here (see «تسجيل تواصل»), but a rep can now create the appointment/visit itself straight from the conversation.
- **Auto-reopen on inbound (new 2026-07-02).** A customer message arriving in a **closed** (resolved/archived) conversation reopens it automatically. The webhook's `bumpConversationRecord` flips the record's `status` back to `active` AND pushes the reopen to Haberchat via `patchChat` (best-effort, logged on failure — required because Haberchat's status is authoritative on the next chats-list sync). The SPA's Realtime `bumpParentFromMessage` mirrors the flip locally for instant UI. Outbound messages never reopen a closed chat.
- **Preference chips (new 2026-07-02).** For each client-linked row, `src/pages/Chats/lib/prefChips.ts#buildClientPrefChips` builds: one chip per `preferred_unit_type` value (bilingual option labels); one `preferred_area` chip ("200–400 م²", one-sided ranges render "200+ " / "≤400 "); and ONE location chip **city · district · element** — country/region intentionally omitted. City resolves from the `location` cascade's city id via the loaded cities/districts records. Districts/elements come from include-polarity `location_items` (inline `district_label` / `element_label`; exclude items skipped). The legacy free-text city / neighborhood / direction fallbacks were removed 2026-09-27 — those values were migrated into `location` / `location_items`, and a direction arrives as the districts of its curated zone. No location → no location chip. Additional districts/elements beyond the first of each collapse into a "+N" suffix.
- **Project pickers prefill from Client Options (2026-09-27).** «Notify officer» and «Register in portal» prefill their project picker when the linked client has exactly one ACTIVE project option (Client Options, `source_type='project'`, not eliminated / not interested; main option first) — read from the store via `clientActiveOptionRefs` in `src/lib/matching/clientOptionIndex.ts`. No option → the picker starts empty (no crash).
- **Detail header** shows avatar + name + status picker (click to resolve/archive/reopen) + phone + kind + last-message-at + a linked-client row or a "Create client" CTA when no phone match was found. **When a client is linked (updated 2026-07-02):** the avatar, the name, and the «عميل مرتبط: name» row are all anchors that open the FULL client record (`/model/clients/:id`) in a **new browser tab** — deliberately not a modal and not same-tab navigation. Below the link row, a **detailed preference-chips row** (`buildDetailedClientPrefChips`) summarizes the client's preferences: unit type(s), budget / bedrooms / area ranges, all location preferences (city, included + excluded districts, element rules like "within X km of …"), max distance, direction, amenities, purchase objective, priority/language settings, and truncated preference notes (full text on hover). Chat labels/tags are deliberately NOT shown in this area (the header labels editor was removed 2026-07-02 — preferences only). **On mobile (<md) the chips row is collapsible (new 2026-07-12):** it hides behind a «التفضيلات (N)» / "Preferences (N)" toggle (chevron rotates when open), collapsed by default so a long preference list doesn't shrink the visible thread; desktop always shows the chips with no toggle.
 (Off while `advertisers` is archived — D47, 2026-09-27.) When the chat's phone matches an `advertisers` record, a gold **«معلن: name»** pill (Megaphone icon) also appears, navigating to `/model/advertisers/:id` — it coexists with the client link / create-client CTA. **When the phone matches a `project_officers` record (new 2026-09-02)** a copper **«مسؤول مشروع: name» / "Project officer: name"** pill (UserCheck icon) appears the same way, navigating to `/model/project_officers/:id` (mobile: `RecordFormModal` overlay) — this is the developer's/marketer's coordinator we message via "Notify officer", now recognized on the inbound side. The officer's saved name also becomes the conversation's display name (ranked below a linked client / saved contact) so the header/list/avatar stop showing the bare number.
- **Message thread** loads the latest 50 messages on mount via the proxy. A "Load older" button at the top paginates backwards using the oldest message's `date` as cursor; scroll position is preserved when older history is appended. **Pinned-to-bottom scrolling (fix 2026-07-04):** the thread stays anchored to the newest message while the user is at the bottom — a `ResizeObserver` on the content + container re-scrolls on any resize, covering media bubbles that swap their fixed-height skeleton for the real (taller) image/video after the authenticated blob fetch, and the whole-thread reflow when the Amiri webfont loads on a cold cache. The scroll container sets `overflow-anchor: none` (this component is the anchoring authority; the browser's native anchoring fired scroll events during reflow that broke the pin). Unpinning requires an actual upward scroll — not just distance from the bottom — and any scroll ending within 60px of the bottom re-pins; reading history is never yanked back down by an arriving realtime message (arrivals only auto-scroll while pinned).
- **Bubbles**: outbound right-aligned copper, inbound left-aligned sand, keyed off `flow` not document direction (an Arabic outbound message still sits on the right in RTL). Text, image (inline preview), video (<video controls>), audio (compact player), document (download chip) all render inline. Quoted replies render as a small chip at the top of the bubble. Ack ticks on outbound: clock = pending, single tick = sent, double tick gray = delivered, double tick copper = read, red alert = failed.
- **System & body-less messages.** Some WhatsApp message types arrive without any text body. **System events** — `notification_template` (WhatsApp system notices, discriminated by `subtype`, e.g. `biz_privacy_mode_init_fb` = "this business works with another company to manage this chat"), `call_log`, `e2e_notification`, `revoked` — render as a small **centered, muted notice** (localized, subtype-aware) rather than a left/right bubble. **Conversational-but-body-less** kinds — `interactive` (button/list messages), `template`, `poll`, `contact` — render as a normal bubble with a friendly labeled icon chip ("رسالة تفاعلية / Interactive message", etc.). This replaced a raw `[interactive]` / `[notification_template]` placeholder. The text of these types genuinely isn't delivered by Haberchat/WhatsApp (verified against the live API), so nothing is extracted — the improvement is purely how they're presented. Haberchat's `subtype` is stored on `chat_messages.subtype` and plumbed through both normalizers, the proxy, and the Realtime merge.
- **Composer**: textarea (Enter sends, Shift+Enter newline, auto-grows to 6 rows), paperclip button (opens native file picker, chosen file shows as a removable chip above the textarea, 10 MB upper bound enforced), schedule (clock) button, send button. Uploads land first, then the message is queued with the returned `mediaFileId`.
- **Scheduled messages (new 2026-07-13; extended to every send surface later the same day).** Every user-facing send surface offers send-now OR schedule via the shared **`SchedulePopover`** — quick presets (in 1 hour, tonight 8 PM (hidden once past), tomorrow 10 AM, tomorrow 8 PM) plus a custom `datetime-local` picker (min = now + 2 min, defaults to +1 h). Confirming sends with a `deliverAt` ISO timestamp; **Haberchat's own delivery queue** (Wassenger-native) holds and fires it, so it delivers even with the app closed. No optimistic bubble is added — the message appears in the thread via the ordinary `message:out` webhook when it actually sends. The surfaces: **(1) open-chat Composer** — clock button next to Send (enabled whenever Send would be); **(2) StartChatModal** — «جدولة» footer button next to Send; the conversation record is still created immediately (the user lands in the chat and sees the scheduled chip), only the first message is queued — this covers every entry point that opens the modal (chats-list +, global composer, client-record WhatsApp button, Follow-up Workspace, Project Finder / Client Options «إرسال للعميل» for projects and market listings, contact-advertiser flows); **(3) template gallery / video ride-alongs** — `sendProjectImageMessages` takes `deliverAt` and staggers each media message +10 s after the text so the queue delivers in order (uploads to Haberchat still happen at schedule time); **(4) SendDocumentModal** (see document-generation.md). Pending scheduled messages for the open chat show as **gold chips above the composer input** (localized "today/tomorrow + time" delivery label, body preview, 📎 marker for media), loaded from Haberchat's queue filtered to the chat's phone (digits-only compare) and refreshed after every schedule/cancel. **More than 2 queued messages collapse into ONE compact summary row** («N رسائل مجدولة (منها M مرفق) — أولها …») — a scheduled listing message (1 text + ~9 photos) no longer buries the thread; the row expands to a max-height scrollable chip list and carries an **«إلغاء الكل» cancel-all**. Each chip has a one-click **cancel** (allowed only while still queued; on failure the strip re-syncs — the message may have already gone out). **Scheduled gallery fan-outs upload CONCURRENTLY** (4-way pool — safe because delivery order comes from the staggered `deliverAt`, not send order; immediate sends stay sequential to preserve order), so scheduling a photo-heavy message no longer holds a long spinner. The proxy rejects only a `deliverAt` that is genuinely in the **past** (30s grace for clock skew) with a clear 400 — a near-immediate future time is valid, because the live WAHA provider delivers from the `scheduled_whatsapp_jobs` queue on a ~3s poll (the manual `SchedulePopover` still enforces ≥1 min itself in the UI). **Fix 2026-09-17:** the old "≥1 min in the future" server rule silently broke **bulk project send**, whose ~4s cadence sits well under a minute, so every text enqueue 400'd with "deliverAt must be at least 1 minute in the future" (`0 queued`); taking longer over a big batch made it worse, since the client cursor (anchored at send-start, advanced by fixed steps) fell behind the enqueue loop's real network time and drifted into the past. Now the server only rejects past times and `enqueueBulkProjectSend` clamps each project's base to `max(cursor, now + START_DELAY)` so a slow loop can never schedule into the past. A strip load failure renders an inline muted "couldn't load scheduled messages + retry" row (console-logged, deliberately not a toast — it would fire on every chat open during a Haberchat wobble).
- **Realtime via webhook.** Every inbound or outbound message from Haberchat fires a webhook. The handler writes the row to `chat_messages` via the service-role Supabase client; Supabase Realtime pushes the INSERT/UPDATE to every browser subscribed to the table. The global subscription lives on `ChatsSplitPage` — active for the entire time the user is under `/model/chats/*` — so the list updates even when the user isn't viewing the specific conversation that got a new message.
- **Optimistic send.** Pressing Send inserts a pending placeholder bubble immediately (clock icon); when the proxy returns the server wid, the placeholder is swapped for a real row with `ack: 'sent'`. Webhook `message:out:ack` events progress the tick through `delivered` → `read`. Failed sends flip the placeholder to red + toast the error.
- **Start new chat (two recipient modes).** `+` in the list header opens a modal with a recipient toggle. **Existing client** (default) runs an advanced client search (see next bullet) and auto-fills the phone (read-only) from the chosen client; if that client has no valid phone the modal shows an inline warning and keeps Send disabled. **Manual number** uses the shared `PhoneInput` (country-code dropdown + number) combined to E.164 via the existing normalization; if the typed number matches an existing client (the same digits-only matcher used for auto-linking, scoped to what the user may see), a **"Matches a saved client: CLT-… · «name» — use this client?"** chip appears and, on click, switches to that client (locked card). The message box supports free text, an inserted template, or a template the user edits; an **Insert template** button opens `TemplatePickerModal` and inserts the template's text into the textarea (empty → inserted directly; non-empty → appended below a blank line, never overwriting). Send stays disabled until BOTH a valid recipient and a non-empty trimmed message exist. On submit a local chat record is created (so it appears in the list immediately), the first message is sent, the conversation is linked to the chosen client (explicit `clientRecordId`) or to a phone-matched client, and the user lands on the new detail pane. Device picker shows only when >1 number is active. **Opened pre-connected from elsewhere (new 2026-06-17):** the Follow-up Workspace's WhatsApp button now opens this same modal instead of handing off to a `wa.me` URL — it passes the current client (`initialClient`, opening straight in existing-client mode, conversation already linked) or, if the follow-up isn't linked to a client, the client's phone (`initialPhone`, opening manual mode pre-filled). So a rep starts the WhatsApp from inside Wassell and the conversation is created + linked here. **Phone canonicalization (fix 2026-06-17):** before deriving the chat `wid`, `startNewChat` runs the typed phone through the shared `normalizePhone()` (`src/lib/phone.ts`) so a bare 9-digit Saudi number (e.g. `554446109`) collapses to the same `+966…` `wid` as the E.164 form — the same person can no longer split into two separate conversations because of how their number was typed (the bug used to mint a stray `+554446109` thread). One pre-existing empty duplicate from before the fix was cleaned up.
- **Advanced client search (in Start New Chat).** The client picker (`ClientPicker.tsx`) reuses the records-table search engine — an "All fields" or single-scoped-field dropdown built from `collectViewFields(clientsModel)`, matched against a prebuilt `buildRecordSearchText` / `buildExpandedFieldSearchText` index normalized with `normalizeForSearch` (case- + Arabic/ASCII-digit-insensitive, resolves dropdown labels + lookup/mirror display values). It honours the user's view-scope on Clients via `useApplyViewScope` (a sales rep only searches their own clients), and renders each result as `client_code • client_name • phone_number`. The business `client_code` (auto_id), display name (card-title field), and phone (first `phone` field) slugs are resolved from the model schema, not hardcoded, so a Builder rename doesn't break it.
- **Client auto-link.** Whenever a chats record is synced, updated via Realtime, or the user saves a clients record, we sweep unlinked chats and try to match each to a client by digits-only phone compare (exact or suffix match, ≥6 digits, derives the phone-field slug from the clients model schema so it's not hardcoded to `phone` vs `phone_number`). Never overwrites an existing link.
- **Status + labels.** Status is editable from the detail header via a 3-way pill (active / resolved / archived), optimistic with revert-on-failure. **Labels no longer have a header editor (removed 2026-07-02** — the header's top area is reserved for client preference chips): the `labels` data key, the `patchChat` labels path, and the Haberchat PATCH endpoint all remain, but there is currently no UI surface for editing them. **Fixed 2026-07-02:** the proxy now calls Haberchat's PER-CHAT endpoints — `PATCH /chat/{deviceId}/chats/{chatWid}/status` body `{status}` and `PATCH …/{chatWid}/labels` body `string[]`. The previous bulk `{action, wids:[…]}` shape was rejected upstream with a 400 on EVERY status change since the feature shipped (the bulk endpoint expects `chats:[…]`, not `wids`, and has no archive action) — the optimistic revert masked it until the Done button surfaced the failure. Haberchat is a whitelabel of Wassenger; the authoritative OpenAPI spec is at `app.wassenger.com/docs/specification`.
- **Unread counts are CRM-owned (fix 2026-07-17).** The webhook increments `unread_count` on every new inbound message; opening the chat detail zeroes it AND persists the zero to Supabase (with a `last_read_at` stamp), so the badge stays cleared across refreshes. While a chat is open, a new arrival is re-cleared immediately (the user is looking at it). Chats-list syncs never overwrite the count — Haberchat's `meta.unreadCount` reflects the PHONE's read state, not this app's, and is only used to seed a conversation the CRM has never tracked.
- **WhatsApp settings live on one tabbed page (2026-09-29).** Settings → «واتساب» (`/settings/whatsapp`) has two tabs: **«الأرقام / Numbers»** (`?tab=numbers`, the connected numbers + the WAHA connection card) and **«المساعد الذكي / AI agent»** (`?tab=ai`, the reply policy). The old `/settings/whatsapp-numbers` and `/settings/whatsapp-ai` URLs redirect to those tabs. **Who can SEE which chats is no longer here** — that is an access question, so the chat-visibility picker moved to Settings → Team & Access → Access levels (see access-control.md); `/settings/whatsapp-permissions` redirects there. Inbound webhook endpoints are NOT WhatsApp settings either — they are a tab of Workflows.
- **Multi-device support.** The Numbers tab of Settings → WhatsApp (`/settings/whatsapp?tab=numbers`) lists every WhatsApp number connected to the Haberchat account, merges with a local overlay for friendly names + default flag + hidden flag. The composer's send-from picker only appears when >1 active number exists; single-number setups silently use the default. **The device overlay (`waDevices`) now loads at app boot (fire-and-forget in `initialize()`), not only when the Settings page is opened (fix 2026-06-17).** Previously `loadWhatsAppNumbers` ran only from the Settings page and the Workflow editor, so a send composer opened in a fresh session before visiting Settings — most often the Follow-up Workspace's in-app WhatsApp button — had an empty `waDevices`, the device-resolution fallback chain bottomed out at `''`, and the send threw "No WhatsApp device configured — set a default in Settings" even when the DB held a correct default. Loading at boot makes the default device available app-wide from the first render.
- **Group / channel v2.** v1 renders group / channel conversations in the list but disables the composer and shows "sending to groups and channels is not yet supported". Inbound group messages are received but lack per-sender rendering.
- **Alternate surface — WhatsApp history inside a client record (new 2026-05-10).** The clients system model has a "WhatsApp History" section that renders the same messages via the new `whatsapp_history` field type (see [clients.md](clients.md)). It uses the same global `subscribeToAllChats` channel and the same `MessageBubble` component, so the visual + Realtime behavior matches the dedicated chats page. The clients-side panel additionally tags each bubble with a "via &lt;our device name&gt;" label so the user can tell which of our numbers handled the conversation when a single client has chatted us across multiple devices. Both surfaces read from the same `chatMessages` slice — no duplicate fetches, no separate state, no risk of drift.
- **Project Message templates (new 2026-06-08).** The chat_templates custom editor has a **Generate project messages** button. It opens a two-step wizard: (1) search + checkbox-select one or more `our_projects` records (search spans name / city / district / unit type / price); (2) review one bilingual message per project. Each project's facts are resolved from the LIVE `all_projects` master (reached via the `our_projects.project` lookup): scalar fields by candidate slug — the live model was Builder-rebuilt with different slugs than the seed (`preferred_city`, `preferred_neighborhoods`, `brochure_url`, `project_location`, `unit_types`) — and the auto-calculated rollups (bedroom/bathroom ranges, price floor) by `computed_kind`. The message is composed **DETERMINISTICALLY** (no AI): the project name on the first line, then one labeled line per PRESENT field, in a fixed order — **no greeting, no closing, no extra text**. Labels are exact; the price label is «الأسعار تبدأ من» / «Prices start from». The project name is a single Arabic text field with no English form, so for the **English** body it's **transliterated** (phonetic — how the brand SOUNDS, not a meaning translation, so «مينا» → «Mena» not «Marina») via `/api/transliterate-name` (Qwen3 30B on Cloudflare Workers AI primary since 2026-07-18, Claude Haiku automatic fallback; cached + dedup'd by `transliterateName`; non-Arabic names like "178" pass through; falls back to the Arabic name if the API is unavailable) — e.g. «مينا 52» → «Mena 52», «الماجدية 178» → «Almajdiya 178». A field with no value is **OMITTED entirely** from the message body (never written as «غير متوفر» / «Not available») — but the preview still shows an amber warning listing what's missing, so the agent knows. Per project the user can edit either body, regenerate (re-compose), skip, or save. Saved templates are `language:'both'`, tagged `project`, and carry `data.project_id` linking to the master `all_projects` record.
  - **Revised message shape + images (2026-06-30).** The composed body **drops the map/location line** and **replaces the brochure** with a link to the project's units on the public website, labeled «الرابط» / «Link» → `https://wassel.re/project?id=<all_projects id>#units` (the `#units` anchor jumps to the units grid). City, district, unit types, and bedroom/bathroom ranges are unchanged. An **«المساحة» / «Area»** line (rounded to whole **م² / m²**) is inserted right after bedrooms (added 2026-06-30). **Since 2026-07-04 (QA-003)** the price and area lines read the AVAILABLE-units-only rollups (`available_price_range` / `available_area_range`) — sold/reserved units never set the quoted starting price or area, and a project with no available units omits both lines. Each saved template also carries `data.project_image_file_ids` — the CRM `files` ids of every image saved on the master (`all_projects.project_images` gallery + `main_image`, deduped, hero first). When a project template is sent, those images are sent as **their own WhatsApp image messages after the text** (multi-image send — see below). `resolveProjectFacts` now also returns `websiteUnitsLink` + `imageFileIds`; `composeProjectMessage` renders the link line and omits location.
  - **Off-plan is ALWAYS disclosed (new 2026-09-15).** A «الحالة» / «Status» line now sits right after the district in every project message: «على الخارطة — التسليم المتوقع أغسطس 2028» / "Off-plan — expected handover August 2028" when the project is still being built and we know the handover month; plain «على الخارطة» / "Off-plan" when we don't (**a handover date is never invented**); «جاهز» / "Ready" when it is finished; and **nothing at all** when the project's status genuinely can't be established — we'd rather say nothing than claim a readiness we can't prove. Whether a project is ready or off-plan decides whether a buyer can move in, get a mortgage, or hit a deadline, so hiding it, burying it, or softening it into "coming soon" is treated as a defect. The answer is read from the project's EXISTING fields — construction status first, then sales status, plus the handover date — by one shared rule (`src/lib/projectMessage/delivery.ts`) that the Project Finder's delivery badge, the deterministic sheet, the AI message and the payment-plan message all share, so no two surfaces can disagree. Three enforcement points back it up: (1) the **AI writer** is told the status is mandatory and is handed the ready-made phrase; (2) an **output guard** rejects an off-plan message whose body doesn't say «الخارطة»/"off-plan", or that drops the handover year when one is known — the send then falls back to the saved template or the deterministic sheet, which always carry the line; (3) a **saved template** for an off-plan project no longer skips the fact-check when it doesn't disclose off-plan status or quotes the wrong handover year. Measured on live data the day it shipped: of 42 our_projects with a saved template, 19 are off-plan and **none** disclosed it; 11 of those had been sending verbatim and now get fact-checked first.
- **Listing Message (new 2026-06-30; ⛔ UNREACHABLE since 2026-09-14 — the market-listings module is archived).** The only entry points to this flow are the `market_listings` record form (now the archived notice), the Project Finder's listing cards and the client-option listing "Send to client" action (all hidden while `MARKET_LISTINGS_ARCHIVED` is on), so no new listing message can be generated; `JobsIndicator` also stops rehydrating listing-message jobs. Already-saved listing templates ("@<ad id>") remain pickable in any chat and still fan out their cleaned photos + videos. Code, endpoints, worker lanes and data are all kept — the description below is the pre-archive behaviour. On a `market_listings` record form a "Generate message" panel produces a reusable WhatsApp message for that listing: an **AI-written, editable bilingual** message (unit type, district, area, bedrooms, bathrooms + amenities extracted from the listing description) AND **every listing photo cleaned of text** (fal `image-editing/text-removal`, which preserves the rest of the photo). Photos clean in parallel on the Fly worker via a per-photo `generation_jobs` (`kind='clean-text'`) fan-out; the modal shows live progress with per-photo + "redo all" buttons. **Approve** saves a `chat_templates` record linked to the listing (`data.listing_id`), holding the text + cleaned `images[]`, and removes any prior message for that listing (one per listing). **The template is NAMED after the listing's Aqar ad id — "@<external_id>" (default since 2026-07-04)** — both the server-side draft and the modal's editable name field default to it (listing title only as fallback for the rare listing without an id), so listing messages are findable by the same @id shown on finder cards and client options. **Approve also REPLACES the market listing's own photos** with the cleaned versions (server-side `apply-to-listing`): per-index, cleaned where available, original kept for any photo whose cleaning failed; the TRUE originals are backed up ONCE to `original_image_urls` / `original_main_image_url` so it's reversible AND so future cleans re-source from the originals (never clean an already-cleaned image). **Reuse-first:** opening the panel for a listing that already has a saved message shows it instead of regenerating. The cleaned photos render in the chat_templates editor. Endpoints: `/api/templates/listing-message` (text), `/api/templates/clean-listing-images` (start/redo the photo jobs). **Since 2026-07-02 the cleaned `images[]` are also SENT:** picking a listing template in an open chat fans them out as their own WhatsApp image messages after the text — same multi-image send as project templates (see below).
  - **Photos are mirrored into our own bucket at scan time (2026-07-29) — cleaning no longer touches Aqar's CDN.** Aqar's Cloudflare blocklists datacenter egress by ASN and began 403-ing Fly's ranges around 2026-07-24; because the cleaning lane downloaded each photo from `images.aqar.fm` before calling fal, **every** photo in the grid showed «فشل», with `data.cleaning[].error = "source re-host failed: source fetch 403"` — nothing to do with fal or Anthropic credit. Measured 2026-07-29 on one image: laptop 200, me-central1 VM 200, Fly bom 200, but Fly sin (4 of 5 deck-worker machines) 403, a freshly-provisioned Fly fra 403, and Fly sjc (the scanner's own region) 403 — so region-hopping is not a fix. Now every market listing's photos are copied ONCE into the public **`listing-photos`** bucket and recorded on the record as `data.image_mirror_map = { "<aqar url>": "<our url>" }`, keyed by source URL so a re-scan is a no-op and only genuinely new photos are fetched. `clean-listing-images` resolves that map at enqueue time into each job's `params.fetch_url`, and the worker hands fal our URL directly — no Aqar download AND no re-host round-trip. **Graceful degradation, not a hard cutover:** a photo with no mirror still falls back to the old download (direct → me-central1 proxy) and enqueues a mirror so the next clean is covered, so the worst case is exactly the previous behaviour. Mirroring is triggered by `records_enqueue_listing_mirror` on insert / image_urls change (the weekly reconcile's price-and-sold updates don't re-fetch anything), and the pre-existing 51k listings are filled in by the deliberately throttled operator script `scripts/backfill-listing-mirrors.mjs` — see the cost note in its header. **The image proxy stays** (it is the only working egress from Fly, so it is what the mirror itself downloads through, and it remains the fallback). Kill switch + per-listing photo cap: `listing_mirror_settings`.
- **Multi-media WhatsApp send is SERVER-SIDE (2026-07-19; was browser-side since 2026-06-30).** A template's images + videos are sent as their own WhatsApp messages after the text. The picker merges the sources into one ordered send list (`templateImageSends` in `TemplatePickerModal.tsx`): a project template's `project_image_file_ids` (CRM `files` ids) and a listing template's cleaned `images[].public_url` + curated `videos[]` (public marketing-assets URLs). `src/lib/projectMessageImages.ts#sendProjectImageMessages(chatWid, refs)` now makes ONE small keepalive POST to **`/api/whatsapp/send-media-batch`** (nodejs, maxDuration 300): the endpoint RLS-checks + service-signs CRM file ids, passes raw URLs through, and sequentially gateway-sends each item — WAHA fetches the bytes itself, so no media flows through the browser or the function. REFRESH-SAFE: once the request lands, the server completes the fan-out even if the tab closes (the old in-tab fetch→upload→send loop died on refresh — live incident 2026-07-19: a listing message's text sent, zero of its media followed). Scheduled sends stagger each item +(i+1)×10s after the text via the scheduled_whatsapp_jobs queue (signed URLs padded to outlive delivery). Best-effort per item: failures are tallied (`{sent, failed, firstError}`) and toasted; the lib never rejects (callers fire-and-forget). The Job Center entry shows the batch in flight. **Batch size (2026-07-22):** send-now batches over 30 items are NOT rejected — the endpoint auto-upgrades them to the `scheduled_whatsapp_jobs` queue (deliverAt = now, 10s stagger) so the Fly worker delivers them with no serverless time limit; the hard ceiling is 120 items and fails loudly (no silent trim). Before this, a 30+-image gallery 400’d wholesale — text sent, zero media (live incident 2026-07-22).
- **Per-conversation SEND LANE (2026-09-07) — a later send never cuts into a gallery still going out.** `src/lib/chat/sendLane.ts` keeps outbound sends to one conversation single-file inside the tab. A send-now media fan-out (`sendProjectImageMessages`) HOLDS the conversation's lane for as long as the batch request is in flight (and, when the server auto-upgraded the gallery to its delivery queue, until the returned `lastDeliverAt` + one stagger); the composer's multi-file fan-out holds it for its loop. Every other send-now to that conversation — a units/unit PDF from the units list (`sendPdfToChat`), a typed reply, a retried bubble — goes through `appStore.sendChatMessage`, which creates the pending bubble immediately, then WAITS for the lane to free before dispatching, refreshing the bubble's timestamp so it sorts after what it waited for. The Job Center entry for a waiting PDF reads «بانتظار انتهاء «إرسال 5 من الوسائط»». Scheduled (deliverAt) sends never touch the lane (they sit in the server queue at explicit times). **Cross-tab (same browser):** every hold is also mirrored into localStorage as its own key (`wassell_send_lane:<chatWid>:<holdId>`, one key per hold so tabs never clobber each other) — a promise hold carries a heartbeat the owning tab refreshes every 4 s and other tabs honor it while the heartbeat is under 20 s old OR its estimated end (~10 s/item) has not passed (the keepalive batch keeps sending server-side even if the owning tab closed); a time hold is honored until its timestamp. Waiting tabs wake on the `storage` event and poll once a second as fallback; dead/corrupt entries are pruned by whichever tab reads them. So a units PDF sent from a SECOND tab still queues behind a gallery started in the first. Boundary: same browser profile only — another device, browser, or operator is not covered (that needs a server-side lane). A send parked in a tab dies with that tab, so a `beforeunload` guard warns while anything is queued. Every hold has a ceiling (6 min for a request, 25 min for a queue timestamp) that force-releases with a `console.error` — a hung request degrades to the old interleaving, never a frozen composer. Before this, a units PDF sent while the project gallery was still going out landed between two photos (live complaint 2026-09-07).
- **Template language choice on insert.** When a chat-template picker (in the open-chat Composer or the Start New Chat modal) lists a template that has BOTH an Arabic and an English body, selecting it does NOT insert immediately — the row expands to an **Arabic / English** choice, and the chosen body is inserted into the composer (never auto-sent). Single-language templates insert directly as before. A small "AR · EN" chip marks dual-language templates in the list.
- **Template classification: Project vs Contact messages (new 2026-07-02).** Every chat template is one of exactly two classifications, stored in `data.category`: **`project`** («رسائل المشاريع» — project/listing messages: recommendations, project details, prices, locations, brochures; all generated project & listing templates belong here) or **`contact`** («رسائل التواصل» — general client communication: greetings, follow-up messages, appointment reminders, check-ins). The picker shows a 3-tab filter (All / Project messages / Contact messages) plus a per-row category badge. Legacy templates without the field are classified at read time by `resolveTemplateCategory` (`src/lib/chatTemplates.ts`): linked to a project or market listing → project, otherwise contact; the migration backfills the same rule into the stored data.
- **Create a template from inside the chat (new 2026-07-02).** The picker's header (and its empty state) carries **«إضافة قالب جديد» / Create new template**, which swaps the modal body to an inline form — classification toggle (Project / Contact, defaulting to the active tab), name, language, Arabic/English bodies, and an optional attachment (uploaded to Haberchat once, same as the full editor). Saving returns to the list with the new template immediately pickable — no navigation away from the conversation. The full-page editor at `/model/chat_templates/:id` remains for tags, project links, and cleaned listing photos.
- **Browse projects & units without leaving the conversation (new 2026-08-18).** A slide-over **Projects & Units browser** (`src/pages/Chats/components/ProjectsUnitsBrowser.tsx`) opens over the thread: full-screen on a phone, an inline-end panel (~600px, RTL-aware) from `md` up so the conversation stays readable behind it. It is an overlay SIBLING of the conversation, so nothing unmounts — the thread keeps its scroll position and Realtime subscription, and the composer keeps the typed draft. The list is **OUR projects only** — every `all_projects` record linked from `our_projects` that the rep may see (`useApplyViewScope`), resolved through the same pure `resolveProjectView` the Projects pages use (STORED rollups, never recomputed), with Arabic-folded search (name / developer / city / district / project code), collapsible city · district · unit-type · price · «available units only» filters, and 60-at-a-time paging. (The old All / Ours / Targeted scope tabs were removed 2026-09-06 — in a chat the rep is always picking something of ours to send, so the browser is our-projects only.) A **List / Map** view toggle sits where the scope tabs were: **List** is the paged rows; **Map** plots the SAME filtered our-projects (all 99 carry `latitude`/`longitude`) on the shared `BaseMapView` (clustering, full-view button, layers overlay — the same surface as the Project Finder + Client Options maps), every pin copper, and a pin click shows a compact card (image · name · location · price · available count) with an **«فتح المشروع» / Open project** button that drills into the same project detail as a list row. Bulk send is a list-mode action (hidden on the map). Rows show the **available-units** price range (falling back to the all-units range, labelled, only when nothing is available), location, developer and available count. Opening a project shows its facts plus the SAME `UnitsInventory` component as the project detail page — unit price, area, bedrooms, bathrooms, floor, price/m² and status, with its unit drawer and compare. On a chat with a linked client the inventory carries that `clientId`, so besides browsing the rep can **Save** an individual unit to the client's options (which also saves its parent project) — via `saveUnitToClient`, the same engine as the finder; see [project-matching-assistant.md](project-matching-assistant.md). Actions reuse the existing paths, never new ones: **«إرسال للعميل»** hands off to `ProjectWhatsAppFlow` (choose saved-vs-AI-rewritten text → pick which linked files to send → the composer — see the top-of-file note), **«إضافة لخيارات العميل»** calls `saveProjectToClient` (writing to the unified **Client Property Options** store — the same engine as the finder + the unit save, so it now shows in the Client Options tab; replaces the retired `addProjectToClient`→`preferred_projects` write), and **«الملف الكامل»** opens `/model/all_projects/<id>` in a NEW TAB. **«إرسال الموقع» / Send location (new 2026-09-07):** when the sheet is open inside a conversation (or scoped to a client with a usable phone), the opened project also carries a **Send location** button that sends the project's map link as ONE plain-text message into that chat — `📍 موقع مشروع <name>` + the link on its own line (WhatsApp renders it as a tappable map preview). The link resolves via `src/lib/projects/projectLocationLink.ts`: the `project_location` URL as saved (a short Google-Maps link is passed through untouched; a bare `lat,lng` becomes a `maps?q=` link), falling back to the record's `latitude`/`longitude`. Two taps on purpose — the first turns the button into «تأكيد الإرسال إلى <client>» + cancel, the second sends — so a stray click never messages a client. It goes through the store's `sendChatMessage`, so it gets the optimistic bubble, waits in the per-conversation send lane behind an in-flight gallery, and surfaces a failed bubble + toast on error. A project with no saved location shows the button disabled with a «لا يوجد موقع محفوظ» tooltip rather than hiding it. The SAME control (shared `src/components/chat/SendLocationButton.tsx`) sits in the **unit drawer** (`UnitDrawer.tsx`, next to «إرسال PDF للعميل») whenever the drawer has a chat context: it sends the UNIT's own link (`location_url` / coordinates on the unit record) and falls back to the parent project's link, labelled `📍 موقع وحدة <code> — مشروع <name>`. On a chat with no linked client the sheet is browse-only. The same browser is reachable from the client-options popup (`ClientOptionsModal` → «تصفح المشاريع»), which hands the screen over to it and takes it back on close — the catalogue counterpart to the finder's client-scored matching. **Send unit/project PDFs (new 2026-08-23).** The units table carries a **«PDF الوحدات (N)»** action and the unit drawer a **«إرسال PDF للعميل»** action: the first builds a branded A4-landscape table of the CURRENT filtered units, the second a single-unit A4 one-pager (facts + floor-plan image). Both are generated client-side (`src/lib/projects/unitsPdf.ts`, html2canvas→jsPDF, JPEG-embedded to keep the file small; image readiness is checked WITHOUT `img.decode()`, which can hang on a backgrounded tab). A confirm dialog (`SendUnitsPdfModal`) shows the recipient + an editable caption, then sends the PDF into the conversation as a `document` via `sendPdfToChat` (`uploadLocalFile` → the store's `sendChatMessage`, which resolves the recipient phone + send-from device and renders the optimistic bubble) — or downloads it. Send only appears when the sheet was opened from a real conversation (`chatWid` present); Download is always available (project detail page included, via the same `UnitsInventory`/`UnitDrawer` `chatPdf`-less path).
- **File picker tabs + video details (2026-09-30).** `ProjectFilePickerModal` renders Photos / Videos / Documents as tabs (first non-empty tab opens; «تحديد الكل» acts on the open tab; send order unchanged). Thumbnails for every CRM file come from ONE `signThumbUrls` call — transformed image for photos, the stored poster (`files.thumb_path`) for videos and PDFs; a hosted video LINK (no files row) shows its own first frame. Video tiles carry `VideoDurationBadge` (`files.duration_seconds`), the file's `ai_description` (two lines, also under the player in the preview), and a transcript button fed by ONE batch `file_video_transcripts` RPC (`src/lib/files/transcripts.ts`) → `TranscriptModal`. No transcript (music-only / not transcribed) → no button. See `docs/prd/files.md` for where posters and transcripts come from.
  - **Send a project's PAYMENT PLANS — as a PDF or as a text message (new 2026-09-15).** The opened project sheet now also shows the project's **Payment Plans** pane — the same component the project page uses — whenever the project actually has a plan menu (the Postgres rollup of its units' plan cards; no menu, no pane). It lists the distinct payment structures offered across the project's units: the %-split (down / during construction / on handover / post-handover), the developer's own plan name and free-text milestone schedule when supplied, how many units offer it, and the price range rolled up from those units — plus the off-plan/ready status line above, because a payment plan is a conversation about timing. An **«إرسال للعميل» / Send to client** button opens a dialog where the rep chooses **PDF file** or **text message**, chooses the document/message **language independently of the app language**, edits the caption (PDF) or the message body (text), and sends to the conversation's customer — or **Downloads** the PDF / **Copies** the text. The PDF is a branded A4 portrait document built in the browser (same header, logo and recipe as the units table and unit one-pager) and goes out through the existing `sendPdfToChat` path (upload → send as a `document`); the text goes through the store's normal send, so it gets the optimistic bubble, waits in the per-conversation send lane behind an in-flight gallery, and surfaces a failed bubble + toast on error. The tab, the PDF and the message are generated from ONE shared resolver, so they can never quote different plans. Outside a conversation (the project page, an all_projects record form) the same pane offers **Download PDF** only, and the dialog says why Send isn't available.
- **A new inbound message pushes the assigned salesperson's phone (new 2026-08-18).** The moment a customer's message lands in `chat_messages`, an AFTER-INSERT trigger (`chat_messages_enqueue_push`, `SECURITY DEFINER`) enqueues «💬 رسالة واتساب جديدة» to the existing `push_outbox` queue and the Fly worker delivers it as Web Push — so a rep with the app closed still hears about it. Body is the client's name plus a preview of the message («صورة» / «رسالة صوتية» / «مستند» … for media); tapping it opens that conversation at `/model/chats/:conversationId`. **Why a trigger and not the webhook:** `chat_messages` is the one place every inbound message lands whatever wrote it (the WAHA webhook, a chats-list sync, a backfill, a future provider), so a webhook-side enqueue would silently miss the other paths. **Recipient resolution**, each rung falling through to the next: the conversation's mirrored `client_owner` → the linked client's own `client_owner` → a phone match against Clients (only when the conversation record doesn't exist yet, because ingest writes the message *before* it upserts the conversation) → the chat's own `owner` assignee. **Nothing resolved means no push — never an error**, and most inbound traffic is on chats with no linked client at all (266 of 436 messages linked over 30 days as of 2026-08-18; 92 of those on a client with an owner), so silence there is intentional. **Bursts collapse**: one `tag` per conversation makes the phone replace the previous alert instead of stacking, and a `dedupe_key` bucketed to 5 minutes of the *message's own* timestamp turns five rapid messages into ONE queue row. Outbound echoes, reactions, WhatsApp system events, and anything dated more than 15 minutes ago (a history import) never enqueue. It also steps aside when the same message is about to fire the follow-up engine's **customer replied** alert for the same rep and client, so one event never buzzes twice. Admin kill switch alongside the other two built-ins at `/profile → الإشعارات` (`push_builtin_settings.whatsapp_inbound_enabled`, defaults ON). Migration: `supabase/migrations/2026-08-18_09_whatsapp_inbound_push.sql`.
- **Task bar + AI-suggested outcome (added 2026-09-27).** Every client-linked chat shows the client's current open follow-up under the header (`ChatTaskBar`): a WhatsApp task first, else the newest open task of any type — the same rule as the AI matcher `_cos_match_open_followup`, so the bar and the suggestion always point at the same task. It stays visible when the header is collapsed. A client message queues ONE `chat_outcome_suggestions` row per client (DB trigger on `chat_messages`, inbound with text, sales line only; a voice note re-queues when its transcript lands); each further message pushes the 3-minute quiet window out. The Fly worker's chat-outcome lane (`runChatOutcomeJob`, DeepSeek, metered in `ai_usage`) reads the last 40 messages and proposes one outcome from the task type's list — or `none` (row → `no_signal`, never shown) when the conversation hasn't decided anything. The bar shows a proposal only while it targets the task on screen. **Since 2026-09-29 the proposal itself lives in the «النتائج» tab of the AI suggestions card**; the bar keeps the task line (type, state chip, due, picked-up-from) and, when a proposal is live, a compact copper chip «✨ نتيجة مقترحة: {outcome}» in place of «تسجيل النتيجة». The chip expands the chat header if it is collapsed, un-collapses the AI card and switches it to «النتائج». «تأكيد النتيجة» opens `CompleteWhatsAppFollowupModal` with the outcome, date and notes pre-filled and the completion time stamped; «اختيار نتيجة أخرى» opens it with nothing selected; ✕ dismisses. Either way the rep's final choice is written back on the suggestion (`chat_outcome_suggestion_resolve`), so `suggested_outcome` vs `confirmed_outcome` is a standing accuracy meter. The modal opened from the bar never resolves the chat (the Done path still does). Nothing touches the follow-up without the rep.
- **Chat outcome readings target WhatsApp tasks only (2026-10-05).** `_cos_match_open_followup` matches the newest open `whatsapp_follow_up`; a client whose only open task is a call gets no reading attached to it (operator: call results are always a person's). Readings that pointed at call tasks were retired.

## User flows
1. **Reply to an inbound message (happy path):**
   1. WhatsApp user sends a message to the Wassell number.
   2. Haberchat fires `message:in:new` webhook → handler writes to `chat_messages` + bumps the parent record.
   3. Realtime pushes the insert; the list row reorders, unread pill appears. Agent clicks it; thread loads history + shows the new message; unread zeroes.
   4. Agent types a reply, presses Enter; the box clears instantly and an optimistic bubble appears (clock) while the send runs in the background — the agent can immediately type the next message. Webhook + Realtime progress that bubble to sent → delivered → read. If the send fails, the bubble turns red («لم تُرسل») with a Retry button and the reason is toasted; the text is never lost.
   5. If the conversation's identity has not finished resolving (no `wid`/`phone` yet, or the device list is still loading), the composer is replaced by a short bilingual notice until it does — the agent cannot fire a send into a half-resolved conversation.
2. **Start a brand-new chat:**
   1. Click `+` in the list header. The modal opens on the **Existing client** tab.
   2. *Existing client:* search the Clients model (all fields or a scoped field), pick a client → its `client_code • name • phone` shows and the phone auto-fills (read-only). *Manual number:* switch tabs, pick a country code + enter the number (any local format works — `+966` default).
   3. Compose: type free text, and/or click **Insert template** to drop a template's text into the box (appended below any existing text) and edit it before sending.
   4. Send → local record created (linked to the chosen client when one was picked, else phone-matched), first message posted; navigation lands on the new detail pane; the webhook echo reconciles the actual wid.
3. **Resolve a finished conversation:**
   1. Open the chat.
   2. Click the status pill → pick "Resolved".
   3. Status updates optimistically; Haberchat receives the `action: resolve` patch; the chat stays in the list but the header badge is gray.
4. **Turn an unlinked chat into a lead (assisted, 2026-07-21):**
   1. A message arrives from an unknown phone. The chat's header shows **«إضافة كعميل»** next to **«إضافة جهة اتصال جديدة»**.
   2. Agent clicks **«إضافة كعميل»** → `LeadIntakeModal` opens with name + phone prefilled → Approve creates the client (the First-Follow-up workflow makes the booking-call task; the relink sweep attaches the chat).
   3. The modal then asks how to start: **Call now** (booking task pulled to NOW — top of My Tasks with the hot-lead countdown) or **Keep in WhatsApp** (booking task cancelled; a "your turn" WhatsApp task is created since the client wrote first).
   4. **The conversation is renamed to the client's name** (list row, header, avatar) and the header shows a «عميل مرتبط» pill — clicking it (or the avatar / name) opens the full client record in a new browser tab — plus the client's detailed preference chips. (The old manual path — the "create client" CTA opening the raw clients form, with the save-hook sweep back-filling `client_link` — still works.)

4b. **Save an unlinked chat as a plain contact (2026-08-10):**
   1. A message arrives from someone who is neither a buyer nor a real-estate office — a supplier, a maintenance contractor, a government office. The header shows **«إضافة جهة اتصال جديدة» / "Add a new contact"**.
   2. Agent clicks it → `ContactIntakeModal` opens with name + phone prefilled; company / position / notes are optional. If that number is already in the address book, the modal says so before saving.
   3. Save → a `contacts` record is created. No client, no follow-up task, no stage.
   4. **The conversation is renamed to the saved name** — list row, thread header and avatar letter all switch from the WhatsApp push name (or the bare number) to it. A **«جهة اتصال»** pill appears in the header (click → the contact record) with a matching chip on the row, and the "Add a new contact" button disappears. The chat stays in the **Other** tab.

4c. **Save an unlinked chat as a project officer (2026-09-02):**
   1. A message arrives from — or was sent by us to — a developer's/marketer's coordinator we arrange client visits with (the receiving end of "Notify officer"). The header shows **«إضافة كمسؤول مشروع» / "Add as project officer"** alongside the "Add as client" / "Add a new contact" buttons.
   2. Agent clicks it → `OfficerIntakeModal` opens with name + phone prefilled; notes optional. If that number is already an officer, the modal says so before saving.
   3. Save → a `project_officers` record is created (`name`, `phone`, `notes`, `is_active: true`). The developer / marketer / projects **coverage** that makes the officer resolvable for a project is filled in later from the officer's own record page — this intake is identity + label only, no coverage.
   4. **The conversation is renamed to the officer's saved name** and a copper **«مسؤول مشروع» / "Project officer"** pill appears in the header (click → the officer record); the "Add as project officer" button disappears. The chat stays in the **Other** tab. Because the tie is a live phone match (nothing written to the chat), once coverage is set the same officer is what `/api/whatsapp/notify-officer` resolves for their projects.
5. **Send an image:**
   1. Click paperclip → pick an image.
   2. Preview chip shows filename + size. Type optional caption. Send.
   3. Upload runs through the proxy (spinner on send button); on success, `sendChatMessage` posts with the returned `mediaFileId` + inferred `kind: 'image'`. Outbound bubble renders the image inline.
6. **Save a client's locations from the chat (2026-09-27):** open a client-linked chat → «تفضيلات العميل» (usually already read; otherwise «اقرأ المواقع») → on the «الأماكن · المحادثة» slide read the place tiles («يريد» / «لا يريد» chips; an amber «مشكوك» chip = the AI's second opinion doubts it — tap it for the reason) → tap any tile that is wrong to untick it → «احفظ المحدد (n)». The client's location preferences now include those places and the Project Finder uses them. Or «تجاهل» to throw the reading away.
7. **Save a client's other preferences from the chat (2026-09-27):** same card, swipe (or tap the pill) to «المواصفات · المحادثة» → check each tile against the customer's quoted words (the saved value is in the tile's tooltip) → untick anything wrong → «احفظ المحدد (n)» (or «تجاهل»). A call's suggestions are their own slides («… · مكالمة ٣١/٨») with the same buttons.
8. **Record the AI-suggested outcome from the chat (2026-09-29):** a client-linked chat with an open task → the task bar shows «✨ نتيجة مقترحة: …» → click it (the header and the AI card open on «النتائج») → read the reasoning / the customer's words → «تأكيد النتيجة» (completion modal with the answer pre-selected) or «اختيار نتيجة أخرى» (modal with nothing selected) → complete; or «تجاهل» if the AI is wrong. Either modal path writes the rep's final choice back on the suggestion.
9. **Empty state (no numbers connected):**
   - The list shows "Conversations will appear here as new messages arrive" and sends fail with "no WhatsApp device configured — set a default in Settings". Admin opens Settings → WhatsApp → Numbers (`/settings/whatsapp?tab=numbers`) → refreshes the list → picks a default.

## Data touched
- **Reads:**
  - `models` (finds the `chats`, `clients`, and `chat_templates` system models), `records` (conversations for the chats model; clients records for auto-link AND the Start New Chat client picker; `chat_templates` records for the template inserter; `followups` records for the Clients-tab **Waiting for reply** filter and the Log-interaction modal's open-task lookup).
  - `chat_messages` via the global Realtime subscription + direct per-chat queries from the `listMessages` proxy pass-through (history loads come from Haberchat directly, not from this table — the table is populated by the webhook only).
  - `whatsapp_numbers` (overlay for device friendly names + default).
  - For a project message / payment-plan send: the `all_projects` master record — including (2026-09-15) its existing `construction_status`, `project_status` and `handover_date` for the ready/off-plan line — plus the project's `units` records for the payment-plan structures and price ranges. **Nothing new is stored for either feature** — no new fields, no new tables; both are read-and-render.
  - Haberchat (via proxy): `GET /chat/{deviceId}/chats`, `GET /chat/{deviceId}/chats/{wid}/sync`, `POST /files`, `GET /chat/{deviceId}/files/{id}/download`, `POST /messages` (optionally with `deliverAt` for scheduled sends), `GET /messages?status=queued&devices=…` (scheduled strip), `DELETE /messages/{id}` (cancel a scheduled message), `PATCH /chat/{deviceId}/chats`, `GET /devices`.
- **Writes:**
  - `records` (chats conversations — id = uuidv5(chat_wid)); `records.data` fields: `wid`, `name`, `phone`, `kind`, `device_id`, `status`, `owner`, `labels`, `unread_count`, `last_message_at`, `last_message_preview`, `last_message_flow` ('in'/'out' — direction of the newest message, stamped by the webhook + the SPA Realtime bump; drives the "Needs reply" filter), `client_link`, `client_owner`.
  - `client_owner` is a **read-only mirror** of the linked client's `clients.client_owner` (a `public.users.id`), kept in sync by DB triggers — NOT user-editable. Its purpose is per-rep chat visibility: because the view-scope engine can't traverse the `client_link` lookup, this stamped field lets an admin scope the chats model per access level with plain conditions — most simply through the one-choice picker in Settings → Team & Access → Access levels («رؤية محادثات واتساب لكل مستوى وصول»; each person's card on the People tab shows the result), or by hand in the access level's chats entry — `client_link is not empty` ("only client-linked chats", hides advertisers/contacts/other) and/or `client_owner equals current user` ("only my own clients' chats"). The mirror is maintained by `records_fill_chat_client_owner` (BEFORE-fill on the chat: pulls the linked client's owner) + `records_sync_chats_on_client_owner` (AFTER-sync on the client: re-stamps linked chats when the client's owner changes / the client is deleted). Off by default — no profile carries the scope until an admin sets it. `chat_messages` + the `whatsapp_ai_*` sidecars already defer to the chats view-scope class (`wassell_chat_scope_class`), so restricting the list restricts message bodies + the live feed too. Migration: `supabase/migrations/2026-08-14_chat_client_owner_mirror.sql`.
  - `chat_messages` (webhook only, via service role — bypasses RLS). Columns include `subtype` (Haberchat message subtype; discriminates system events for rendering).
  - `push_outbox` (one row per inbound-message alert, written by the `chat_messages_enqueue_push` trigger — never by the app). Reads `push_builtin_settings.whatsapp_inbound_enabled`, the conversation record's `client_owner` / `client_link` / `name` / `owner`, the linked client's `client_name` / `client_owner`, and the client's open `followups` (only to avoid double-notifying alongside `customer_replied`).
  - `whatsapp_numbers` (admin, on Settings → WhatsApp → Numbers).
  - `profiles.model_permissions` (the chats entry's view-scope) — only from the chat-visibility picker in Settings → Team & Access → Access levels.
  - The client's `location_items` — ONLY through the «تفضيلات العميل» card's Save tap → `POST /api/geo-preference/review` (confirm / edit; merged via `record_save`, one `geo_pref_review_audit` row). «اقرأ المواقع» itself (`POST /api/geo-preference/chat-card`) writes only the geography ability's own tables (`geo_pref_evidence` / `_relations` / `_checkpoints` / `_proposals`), never a client record.
  - The client's `preferred_unit_type` / `purchase_objective` / `preferred_amenities` (union with the fresh value, re-validated against the live clients schema options) and `budget` / `preferred_area` / `preferred_bedrooms` (replaced) — ONLY through the preference section's Save tap → `POST /api/client-prefs/review` (`recordSaveWithRetry` → `record_save`).
  - `client_pref_proposals` (the preference agent's pending proposal per chat + client; `superseded` / `saved` / `dismissed`) and `chat_read_state` (read watermarks + lease) — server-side, service role, by the chat auto-read (cron / open / manual).
  - Call audit (2026-09-29): `client_pref_proposals` rows with `source='call'`, `call_id`, `call_at`, `trigger='call_audit'`, stored under `chat_wid = 'call:<call id>'` (one pending per call); `call_pref_audit` (one ledger row per call — running / done / skipped / failed, max 3 attempts) via the `call_audit_candidates` / `call_audit_claim` / `call_audit_finish` RPCs. Reads `call_logs` (duration, hang-up, diarized `transcription.words`) and the `phone_calls` record's `client_link`.
  - Places from calls (2026-09-29, `supabase/migrations/2026-09-29_02_call_audit_geo.sql`): `call_pref_audit` gains `geo_status` / `geo_reason` / `geo_proposal_id` / `geo_attempts` (written by `call_audit_geo_finish`; a geo-only pass leases the call with `call_audit_geo_claim`); `call_audit_candidates` returns `needs_prefs` / `needs_geo`. The geography pass writes what the chat card writes (geo evidence / relations / checkpoint / one pending `geo_pref_proposals` row + the verifier opinion). The card reads `call_pref_audit.geo_proposal_id` → `geo_pref_proposals` → `loadChatCard(call id)`. Saving goes through `/api/geo-preference/review` (fill-empty-only for these proposals).
  - Reads `chat_messages.kind` / `transcript` / `transcript_status` / `media_saved` (inbound voice-note transcripts) for the auto-read and the card's voice badge.
  - `followups` records **server-side via the activity-bridge reconcilers** (`reconcile_outbound_whatsapp` / `reconcile_inbound_whatsapp`, called from the webhook with the service role): waiting-state stamps, reach-out-task cancellations, and bridge-created tasks (`creation_source` = `outbound_whatsapp` / `inbound_whatsapp`).
  - `clients` + `followups` records from the two header modals via the normal store `saveRecord` (Lead intake: new client + booking/WhatsApp task adjustments; Log interaction: task completion or create-then-complete — outcome workflows fire on these saves).
  - `contacts` records from the header's "Add a new contact" modal via the normal store `saveRecord` (`name`, `phone`, optional `company` / `position` / `notes`). Nothing is written to the chat record — the chat↔contact tie is recomputed live by phone match on every render.
  - `project_officers` records from the header's "Add as project officer" modal via `saveRecord` (`name`, `phone`, `notes`, `is_active: true`; coverage — developer/marketer/projects — added later on the record). Like contacts, nothing is written to the chat record — the chat↔officer tie is a live phone match.
  - `clients` + `client_attributions` records **server-side via `mos_capture_ad_acquisition`** (SECURITY DEFINER, called from the webhook on an ad-resolved opening inbound): find-or-create the client (source «ترويج», `creation_source='ad_inbound'`) + append one first/latest-touch paid-ad attribution row. The ledger row stores only ids + when + how — no marketing data is copied onto the client. See [marketing-workspace.md](marketing-workspace.md).

### On a phone (2026-10-05)

- **An open conversation is full-screen.** On phones the app's top bar is hidden
  while a conversation is open (`AppLayout` → `Header hideOnPhone`); the chat's
  own header (back arrow, name, call, actions, ⋯) is the only bar, and the split
  takes the whole visual viewport. The list keeps the app bar.
- **The opened page loads first.** When the app starts on `/model/<name>` (a rep
  opening a chat from the home-screen icon or a push), that model's rows are
  fetched on their own alongside wave 1, so the chat list fills in ~1 s; wave 1
  still replaces them with the complete set when it lands (`appStore` init,
  mark `init:route-records-landed`).
- **Dead media is remembered.** A file the gateway answers 404/410 for is gone
  (WhatsApp drops old media); it shows «مرفق قديم غير متاح» and is not requested
  again this session (`MessageBubble` `goneMedia`).
- **Finger-sized controls.** Every chat control a phone taps is ≥ 32px (the
  status pill keeps its look and gets an invisible larger tap ring); desktop
  sizes are unchanged (`md:` prefixes).
- **Checked by a crawler.** `npm run phone-check:chats` (`scripts/phone-check-chats.mjs`)
  repeats this check: sideways scroll, things cut off, buttons a pop-up leaves
  off-screen, text boxes an iPhone zooms into, small taps, slow screens, errors
  and failed requests, with screenshots. It never sends or saves: every
  non-read request is answered by the script, and send / delete buttons are
  never pressed.

## Key files
| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `src/pages/Chats/components/AiActivityPanel.tsx` | The AI activity cards shown instead of the composer in AI-answered client chats (2026-10-05). |
| `src/pages/Chats/lib/aiActivity.ts`, `src/pages/Chats/lib/aiActivityText.ts` | Shapes + `useAiActivity` hook; plain-language chips / events shared with the client AI timeline. |

| `src/pages/Chats/components/ProjectMessageComposeStep.tsx` | **New 2026-09-02.** The per-project message compose step (saved-vs-AI text, fact-check, language toggle, save-as-template) extracted from `ProjectWhatsAppFlow` so the single-project flow AND `BulkProjectSendFlow` share ONE implementation. Renders its own modal; `onAccept({ text, sendLang })`. |
| `src/lib/chat/sendLane.ts` | **New 2026-09-07.** Per-conversation send lane: `holdSendLane(chatWid, label, promise | {until})` / `waitForSendLane(chatWid, onWaiting)` / `isSendLaneBusy`. Held by `sendProjectImageMessages` (send-now galleries, extended by the batch endpoint's `lastDeliverAt`) and the composer's multi-file fan-out; awaited inside `appStore.sendChatMessage` for every send-now (bubble first, dispatch after the lane frees; `laneBypass` for holders). Cross-tab via one localStorage key per hold + heartbeat (`estimatedMs` = how long other tabs honor a hold whose owning tab closed); `storage`-event wake + 1 s poll. Hold ceilings force-release with `console.error`; a `beforeunload` guard warns while a send is parked. Tests: `src/lib/chat/__tests__/sendLane.test.ts` (single-tab + across-tabs). |
| `src/pages/Chats/components/BulkProjectSendFlow.tsx` + `src/lib/projects/bulkProjectSend.ts` | **New 2026-09-02; async 2026-09-05.** Bulk project send (one client, many projects). The wizard walks each project { `ProjectMessageComposeStep` → `ProjectFilePickerModal preselect="bulk"` } → review (reorder/remove) → Send all. `enqueueBulkProjectSend(recipient, plan)` **enqueues the whole plan to the backend `scheduled_whatsapp_jobs` queue and returns immediately — the rep never waits for delivery.** It schedules each project **text → PDF → pictures** on a staggered `deliver_at` schedule at a uniform ~4s cadence (text at `base`, media at `base + k·4s` via `send-media-batch`'s `staggerSeconds` override, next project one tick later) via the existing scheduled-send primitives (`sendChatMessage`/`startNewChat` with `deliverAt`; `sendProjectImageMessages` with `deliverAt` → `send-media-batch`); the Fly worker delivers in the background in deliver_at order. Ordering is now deliver_at staggering (the same cadence a scheduled single-project gallery uses), **not** the old sent-gating that forced the rep to watch each send. Recipient is an existing `chatWid` or a `PickedClient` (a new chat is established + linked by `startNewChat` on the first enqueue). Queued messages are visible + cancelable in the conversation's scheduled-message strip. Entry points: `ProjectsUnitsBrowser`, `ProjectFinderPage`, `ProjectsListPage`. |
| `api/templates/project-message-ai.ts` + `src/lib/projectMessage/client.ts` | **New 2026-08-23.** Endpoint that AI-rewrites a project's bilingual WhatsApp message from the WHOLE `all_projects` record + an authoritative facts block (frozen-geo place names + AVAILABLE-only price/area per QA-003), force-tooled to `{body_ar, body_en}` and gated (mangled place name / invented ≥6-digit number → 502, UI keeps the saved template). ### Owner alerts — in-app + device, on every inbound message (added 2026-08-31)
When a client's WhatsApp message arrives, the client's **owner** (`client_owner` — the Sales Consultant, mirrored onto the conversation record) is alerted three ways, wherever they are:

- **Device push (existing).** The `chat_messages_enqueue_push` trigger enqueues a `push_outbox` row targeted at the owner; the Fly worker sends an encrypted Web Push — reaches the owner's phone/laptop even with the app closed. Only reaches devices that opted in.
- **In-app toast (`src/components/WhatsAppOwnerAlerts.tsx`).** An app-level watcher on the live `records[chats]` slice (kept current app-wide by the realtime `records` channel) fires a toast the instant a chat **you own** gets a new **inbound** message — from any page. Suppresses the local OS `Notification` when this device is already push-subscribed (the push covers the OS layer; the toast is not a duplicate of it). It **replaces** the old `SalesNotifications` "customer replied — your turn" toast (which fired off a follow-up flip — an indirect proxy that double-buzzed the owner); hot-lead alerts stay in `SalesNotifications`.
- **Header bell (`src/components/WhatsAppOwnerBell.tsx`).** A persistent bell in the top bar with a badge + dropdown of "clients waiting for your reply" — every conversation you own whose customer messaged last and is still unread. Pure projection of the in-memory chats slice (no new table). Opening a row marks it read and deep-links to the thread, dropping it from the list.
- **Push auto-prompt (`src/components/PushAutoPrompt.tsx`).** Because a push only reaches a device that opted in — and most reps never visited `/profile` to turn it on — this asks once per device, on the rep's first interaction after login (the gesture browsers require for `Notification.requestPermission()`). Engages only from the `default` permission state; never re-prompts a denial, never nags. `/profile` (`NotificationSettings`) remains the manual on/off + repair surface.

The owner predicate and name/url resolution shared by the toast and bell live in `src/components/whatsappOwner.ts` so the two surfaces can't drift.
