## WhatsApp AI reply agent (basic + full) — added to this PRD 2026-08-24
There are TWO responders, selected by **`whatsapp_ai_settings.responder_mode`** (`basic` default | `agent`):

**BASIC (default, 2026-08-24) — NOT a Claude session.** `POST /api/whatsapp/basic-reply` (node, secret-gated) answers first-touch messages in ~1s with **deterministic pattern rules + a single Kimi fallback** — no agentic session, no queue. The WAHA webhook calls it on every new inbound. Flow: classify (digit-folded, so «مينا ٥٢»=«مينا 52») → greeting / project-sheet / 3-question qualify / honest-"no" / hand-off; the ambiguous tail is one constrained **Kimi K3** call that can only pick the same menu (never free-form, never invents facts); anything real hands off. It reuses the existing endpoints — project sheet via `/api/templates/project-message`, send via `/api/whatsapp/ai-send`, hand-off note via `/api/whatsapp/ai-notify`. Ruleset derived from the last 100 conversations (≈54% canned Meta-ad-lead openers like «مهتم بمشروع مينا 52», ≈24% greetings, a few FAQ, ~5% B2B spam). **Why not a skill/Claude session:** a skill only runs inside a full Claude Code session, which measured 60–510s per reply and could wander off-script (it once produced a 510s market study instead of a card) — the wrong engine for basic replies.

**AGENT (`responder_mode='agent'`) — the heavy "Saad" Claude session.** The `wassel-wa-agent` Fly worker spawns a headless Claude Code session per conversation (`WA_SKILL` selects `whatsapp-reply` = full Saad who searches projects, sends a map, qualifies over turns). `basic-reply` delegates to it via `whatsapp_ai_enqueue` when this mode is set. The `whatsapp-basic-reply` SKILL.md is now retained only as the written spec of the rules the endpoint implements — it is NOT the basic executor anymore.

**Specialized search when nothing fits (2026-10-05).** Each agent turn's state carries a SPECIALIZED-SEARCH CHECKLIST (`requestChecklistLine` in `api/_lib/salesAgent/savedProfile.ts`, from the client's saved data by the same rule as the request form: unit type, at least one district, one of budget / bedrooms / size). Brain rule 10a: when nothing fits and the customer won't take the closest, say we don't have it now and will do a specialized search («نسوي لك بحث خاص»), ask in ONE message for only what is still missing (the one exception to one-question-per-message; a region is not a district), then confirm the search without a timeframe or promise. Dry-run checked on 2026-10-05: a client with type + districts was asked only for budget or bedrooms; a chat with no profile was asked for districts and budget; after «5 غرف» the agent confirmed the search; an ordinary «شقة 3 غرف شمال الرياض» still searched (13 projects) and narrowed as before.

**Ad-lead questions go to the agent (2026-10-05).** In the ad-click branch of `basic-reply.ts`, an ambiguous opener is still classified by Kimi first, but only `no_service` and `not_customer` (a vendor / job seeker / wrong number / spam — new action) stop there with the fixed hand-off line. A real customer question or complaint (`kimi_handoff`) starts the sales agent (`startAgentConversation`, source `ad_project` with the ad's project, answering now) whenever the agent is allowed for this client and its scope is `all`; a Kimi outage still falls through to the ad's project. Before this, «فين موقعكم بأي حي» on a resolved يمام بارك 14 ad got «بيتواصل معك زميلي» and no agent turn.

**When it may answer is policy, not code:** the `whatsapp_ai_should_reply` gate (kill switch + schedule + don't-talk-over-a-human + per-chat reply cap + manual takeover) is edited by admins at **Settings → WhatsApp → AI agent** (`/settings/whatsapp?tab=ai`, `WhatsAppAiPage`; the old `/settings/whatsapp-ai` redirects there) which reads/writes the singleton `whatsapp_ai_settings` (RLS: admin-write, all-read) via `api/whatsapp/ai-settings.ts`. The schedule is governed by **`schedule_mode`** (added 2026-08-24): `always` (24/7), `inside_hours` (the days/hours ARE the agent's active window — it replies during them, including the team's working hours, which is how the basic bot clears simple ad-lead messages while reps take the real ones), or `outside_hours` (legacy: reps cover the window, the agent covers nights/weekends). The days/hours pickers mean "when the agent replies" under `inside_hours` and "when the team covers" under `outside_hours`; `always` hides them. **`stop_forever_after_human`** (default TRUE, added 2026-08-24): once any human rep replies in a chat, the autonomous agent stays out of it **permanently** (not just for `human_quiet_hours`) — an explicit manual handover (`ai_managed`) is the only thing that reopens it; turn it off to fall back to the hours-window behavior. The master kill switch (`is_enabled`) ships **off** — nothing reaches a real customer until an admin turns it on. Sends go through `api/whatsapp/ai-send` (re-checks the gate, enqueues to the scheduled-send queue, records the audit row).

**Project sheets are deterministic + guarded — and now ship the brochure + top photos (updated 2026-09-06).** When the **basic responder** (`api/whatsapp/basic-reply.ts`) classifies a named-project lead it calls `sendProjectViaAiFlow` (`api/_lib/aiSendProject.ts`) in its FAST mode (`allowAi:false`, `onlyOurProjects:true`) so it sends the SAME full package a rep does — the marketing message **plus one brochure + the top 3 photos**. The TEXT is the project's saved message when its numbers already match current data (`savedMessageMatchesCurrentFacts`, no model call) else the deterministic `composeProjectMessage` sheet, so it still quotes the **available-only** price range (QA-003 — never a sold unit's price) and the webhook is held only for fast DB work (no ~40s model call). **Since 2026-09-15 both sides of that choice disclose off-plan status:** the deterministic sheet always writes the «الحالة» / «Status» line, and a saved message that fails to disclose it (or quotes the wrong handover year) for an off-plan project no longer qualifies for the "numbers already match" shortcut. The media rides the scheduled queue after the text. **Before 2026-09-06 the basic bot sent the text sheet only** — via `POST /api/templates/project-message` (the no-LLM `composeProjectMessage` sibling of `project-message-ai`), which with `wa-agent/tools/project.mjs` remains for any caller that wants just the plain sheet. Endpoint auth is dual: shared `WHATSAPP_AI_SECRET` (headless, service-role) or a user JWT (RLS-gated).

**The bot can also send the FULL rep package** (added 2026-09-06) — the marketing message **plus the project brochure and its top photos**, in one call — the same thing a human rep sends through `ProjectWhatsAppFlow` (compose → files → chat). `POST /api/whatsapp/ai-send-project` (secret-gated, node, service-role) wraps the shared orchestrator `api/_lib/aiSendProject.ts`, and the runner calls it via `wa-agent/tools/project-flow.mjs`. The skill uses this as the default action for a named-project lead; `project.mjs` stays for the plain text-only sheet. What it does, in order: (1) **MESSAGE** — resolves the text the SAME way reps do (`ProjectMessageComposeStep`'s order): the project's **saved** message (`chat_templates.project_id`), sent verbatim when its numbers already match (`savedMessageMatchesCurrentFacts`, no AI) else **fact-checked** to current numbers, → a fresh **AI rewrite** when there's no saved message, → the **deterministic sheet** as a floor. It never sends a knowingly-stale saved message: if the fact-check/AI call fails it falls back to the sheet (guaranteed-current numbers), not the un-corrected copy. The AI generate/fact-check is the shared `api/_lib/projectMessageAi.ts` (extracted from `project-message-ai.ts` so both the JWT endpoint and this service-role flow share one guarded implementation). The `allowAi` flag gates that step: the endpoint/agent tool run with `allowAi:true` (the full saved→fact-check→AI→sheet order); the fast **basic responder** runs with `allowAi:false`, which skips every model call — saved-message-when-current, else the deterministic sheet — so it never holds the webhook on a ~40s generation. (2) **FILES** — the same **bulk default** the rep picker pre-checks: exactly **one brochure + the top 3 photos**, floor plans excluded, resolved from the project's `file_links` and ordered documents → photos (reusing the pure `buildPickerItems`/`defaultBulkSelection`/`orderSelectedRefsBulk`). Photos always come from the project's linked files (top 3) — there is no saved gallery. (3) **DELIVERY** — TEXT via `enqueueAiReply` (gate re-check + device resolve + audit) at ~now, then each media item into the `scheduled_whatsapp_jobs` queue staggered +4s, so the customer sees text → brochure → photos; a wassel-files object is handed to the worker as a re-signable `wt_` ref (signed URLs expire before a staggered delivery). It returns as soon as everything is ENQUEUED. If the reply gate blocks (a human took over), the text is not sent and no media follows. Partial media is success — an un-sendable file is skipped, the job fails only if the text can't be queued.

**AI notifications feed.** When the agent hands off (or wants attention) it posts an operator-facing note via `wa-agent/tools/notify.mjs` → `POST /api/whatsapp/ai-notify` (secret-gated, service-role insert) into the `ai_notifications` table. Operators read it in **My Tasks → "AI notifications"** — see [sales-rep-workspace.md](sales-rep-workspace.md). Severity is `warning` / `action` / `info`; rows link back to the chat.

## Open questions / known limitations
- **One WhatsApp account, one gateway (operational rule, 2026-07-27).** WhatsApp allows several linked companion devices, and a second live WAHA session on the same number is not a harmless spare: it re-reports every message under its own addressing mode, which used to double the rows and split the delivery receipts. Ingest now merges those copies on the message hash, but the duplicate gateway still burns a companion slot and races the real one. **After any re-pair, unlink the old device from the phone** (WhatsApp → Linked Devices). The webhook logs `events from UNREGISTERED session "<name>"` when one is talking to us.
- **AI reply session state is now a Fly volume (2026-07-27).** `wassel-wa-agent` mounts `wa_agent_state` at `/home/agent/.claude` so the Claude CLI's conversation store survives a deploy. Without it every machine replacement wiped it, `--resume` failed with "No conversation found with session ID", and each customer message restarted the agent cold — the visible symptom was the agent contradicting itself and re-asking what it had already been told. If the volume is ever detached, that regression returns silently.
- **A restart used to wedge a chat permanently (fixed 2026-07-27).** `WORKER` is derived from `FLY_MACHINE_ID`, which survives a restart, so a job killed mid-flight was claimed by the same id the new process reports — and `claude_jobs_recover_orphaned` deliberately skips same-owner jobs. The job sat in `running` forever, and `whatsapp_ai_enqueue` debounces on any pending/running job for that chat, so that customer could never get another AI reply. The runner now reclaims its own stale jobs at boot (`recoverOwnStaleJobs`).
- **Owner assignment.** The header doesn't expose an "owner" picker yet — requires a Haberchat-agent-id ↔ Wassell-user mapping table that doesn't exist. Haberchat's `owner` field round-trips via the `chat:update` webhook but the app treats it as opaque.
- **Groups and channels.** v1 displays them in the list but disables the composer and doesn't render per-sender labels in group threads. Revisit when real-estate teams need group replies.
- **Ack tier gating.** `message:out:ack` events only fire on Haberchat Business / Enterprise plans. On lower tiers, outbound bubbles stay at "sent" (single tick) forever. Consider inferring "delivered" from a tier check instead of always waiting.
- **Records table is now on Realtime (resolved 2026-05-07).** Phase C.1 added `records` to the `supabase_realtime` publication at `REPLICA IDENTITY DEFAULT` (PK only) — the global `RealtimeOrchestrator` channel for `records` refetches the full row by id when a chats parent record is bumped, so a different browser that missed the window now sees the updated `last_message_preview` live. The earlier "scoped out as too noisy" assessment was reversed during the architecture refactor; per-table kill switches (`localStorage.wassell_realtime_disabled='1'` or `VITE_REALTIME_RECORDS=off`) provide rollback if it becomes a problem.
- **Existing `wa_*` tables.** Schema still has the pre-existing `wa_conversations` / `wa_leads` / `wa_errors` scaffolding for a never-shipped autonomous AI replier. Marked DEPRECATED but not yet dropped. Safe to remove in a future migration once confirmed empty.
- **Deleted / edited messages.** Haberchat's `message:update` event isn't handled — edits and poll votes are ignored. Not urgent for the v1 sales workflow.
- **Status "operative".** Haberchat sometimes returns device status strings we don't map (e.g. `operative`). The UI falls back to showing the raw value. Add missing entries to `statusLabel` as they surface.
- **Secret in webhook URL.** Per Haberchat's recommendation we accept the shared secret as a query param. That value ends up in Haberchat's delivery log but not in browser history. If Haberchat later exposes a HMAC-signed header scheme, prefer it.
- **Off-plan disclosure depends on the project's status fields being kept current (2026-09-15).** The rule reads the project's construction status, sales status and handover date; a project whose status fields are ambiguous shows no status line at all — deliberately, because claiming "ready" wrongly is worse than saying nothing, but it also means a genuinely off-plan project with unmaintained fields will not disclose. Likewise a handover date is never estimated: no date on the record means the message says «على الخارطة» with no timing. Both are data-entry responsibilities the code can't infer.
- **Payment plans send at project level only (2026-09-15).** The PDF and the message quote a price RANGE across the units on each plan plus a "price varies by unit" note; there is no per-unit payment-plan send, and the plans pane appears only where the project has a stored plan menu (the rollup of its units' plan cards).
- **Template media in Start New Chat (limitation, 2026-06-08).** Inserting a `chat_templates` template into the *new-chat* composer carries only its TEXT — a template's media attachment is dropped for the first message of a brand-new conversation (the `startNewChat` send path is text-only; the in-conversation `Composer` does support template media). Pick the client/number, send the text, then attach media from the conversation's composer if needed. Threading media through `startNewChat` is a future enhancement.
